```bash
for fn in $({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s' "$wv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | sed "s/'[^']*'//g" | grep -oE '[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*[[:space:]]*\(' | sed 's/[[:space:]]*($//; s/($//' | tr -d '(' | sort -u); do
  case "$fn" in length|ifnull|coalesce|trim|lower|upper|abs|cast|IN|in|EXISTS|exists) : ;; *) echo "FAIL: where_sql function not in whitelist (length/ifnull/coalesce/trim/lower/upper/abs/cast): $fn"; exit 1 ;; esac
done
for ident in $({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s' "$wv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | sed "s/'[^']*'//g" | grep -oE '[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*' | sort -u); do
  case "$(printf '%s' "$ident" | tr 'A-Z' 'a-z')" in
    working_dir|path|paths|description|message|summary|command|text|metadata_json|redaction_report|session_id|provider_session_id|parent_session_id)
      echo "FAIL: where_sql references excluded column (ADR-DC-03): $ident"; exit 1 ;;
  esac
done
et=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^(- |\* )?event_type：[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]/{ sub(/^(- |\* )?event_type：[[:space:]]*/, ""); print; exit }')
case "$et" in *[!A-Za-z0-9_]*|'') echo "FAIL: event_type not a bare identifier: $et"; exit 1 ;; esac
etset=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$sec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^### 事件类型闭集$/{f=1;next} /^### /&&f{exit} f' | awk '/^\|/{print; d=1; next} d{exit}' | awk -F'|' 'NR>2 && NF { v=$2; gsub(/^ +| +$/,"",v); print v }')
{ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$etset" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -qFx "$et" || { echo "FAIL: event_type $et not in 事件类型闭集"; exit 1; }
tb=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^(- |\* )?table：[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]/{ sub(/^(- |\* )?table：[[:space:]]*/, ""); print; exit }')
case "$src" in
  agent_session) ftbl=agent_session; fwh="state IN ('condensed','stopped') AND scope_state = 'scoped'"; fet=session_lifecycle ;;
  'operation v2') ftbl=operation; fwh="status IN ('success','failed','partial','aborted') AND format_version = 2"; fet=operation_record ;;  # R78-P1-1：operation_v2 迁移 `2026090101_operation_v2.sql:14` 的 format_version 默认 2 且无约束，version 3 行不得计入（须显式 `format_version = 2`）
esac
[ "$tb" = "$ftbl" ] || { echo "FAIL: table drifted from frozen value $ftbl: $tb"; exit 1; }
[ "$wv" = "$fwh" ] || { echo "FAIL: where_sql drifted from frozen value (R62 写死；合法 SQL 形态的改写同样拒绝): $wv"; exit 1; }
[ "$et" = "$fet" ] || { echo "FAIL: event_type drifted from frozen value $fet: $et"; exit 1; }
echo "OK: predicates for $src well-formed"
```

### A.7 `a7_rules_guard.sh`（精炼规则守卫；DC-08 用）

可选参数 `$1` = 目标文件（默认 `docs/development/plan/issues/468.md`）。核验：解析在全文级围栏感知视图（先对全文剔除 fenced code block——``` 与 ~~~ 两种形态、含缩进围栏（开/闭均至多 3 空格缩进，4+ 空格缩进按 CommonMark 属缩进代码块、不是围栏；反引号开围栏的信息串含反引号时整行不算围栏），按开启字符与长度配对闭合（结束围栏须同字符、长度 ≥ 开启、同样至多 3 空格缩进且无尾随非空白，不配对的围栏行视为内容）——剔除处注入非管道边界行 `##FENCE##` 防跨围栏拼接，再定位节/子节/表）上进行；「契约草案」节含 heading 恰为 `### 精炼规则` 的小节（字面精确匹配）；**前缀行计数为两段式（R65）：** 先按行首前缀统计全部同名行（含空值行）恰为一行，再核验前缀后含非空白字符——只数非空行会漏掉重复的空值机器行（如追加一行 `- keep_sql：`）；**行首锚定的精确定义（R66-P1-2，与 A.6/A.13 同口径）：** 机器行须顶格书写，或顶格以 `- ` / `* ` 列表标记起首（正则 `^(- |\* )?`）——四空格及以上缩进的内容属缩进代码块、A.12 视图予以保留，缩进示例中的同名行不得冒充或干扰机器行计数与取值；小节含行首锚定、唯一、前缀后含非空白字符的「去噪：」「归一：」「聚合：」三行且各行含「逐字节相同」；小节含 `collect.max_rows`（恰一条赋值行且整行恰为 `collect.max_rows = 10000`，A.13/A.14 消费该值）与「最大字节」字面；含行首锚定、唯一、前缀后含非空白字符的「denoise_sql：」「normalize_sql：」「aggregate_sql：」「keep_sql：」四行（A.13 消费为实例化管道；normalize/aggregate 片段须含 `_v` 引用输入；keep_sql 为去噪前过滤的行级保留谓词；三片段经字符集白名单与非确定性函数排除——`random`/`datetime`/`julianday`/`unixepoch`/`changes`/`last_insert` 等拒绝；**冻结比对（R63；R65 补 denoise 一条使散文与脚本一致）：** `denoise_sql：` 值须逐字为 `DISTINCT`、`normalize_sql：` 值须逐字为 `trim(_v)`、`aggregate_sql：` 值须逐字为 `_v`、`keep_sql：` 值须逐字为 `repo_id = (SELECT value FROM config_kv WHERE key = 'libra.repoid' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1)`——形态合法但取值被改写的漂移（如 `repo_id = repo_id` 重言式）同样拒绝）。

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
f="${1:-docs/development/plan/issues/468.md}"
[ -f "$f" ] || { echo "FAIL: plan file missing"; exit 2; }
fv=$(bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a12_fence_view.sh" "$f")
sec=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^## 契约草案$/{ in_s=1; next } /^## / && in_s { exit } in_s')
[ -n "$sec" ] || { echo "FAIL: contract section missing"; exit 1; }
rules=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s' "$sec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^### 精炼规则$/{f=1;next} /^### /&&f{exit} f')
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$sec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cFx '### 精炼规则')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: rules subsection heading not exactly once"; exit 1; }
[ -n "$rules" ] || { echo "FAIL: rules subsection heading missing (exact: ### 精炼规则)"; exit 1; }
for pre in '去噪：' '归一：' '聚合：'; do
  all=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cE "^(- |\* )?${pre}" || true)
  [ "$all" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: rule $pre total lines $all != 1 (empty-value duplicates counted too)"; exit 1; }
  c=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cE "^(- |\* )?${pre}[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]" || true)
  [ "$c" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: rule $pre value empty"; exit 1; }
  { trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -E "^(- |\* )?${pre}[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]" | grep -q '逐字节相同' \
    || { echo "FAIL: rule $pre lacks deterministic non-blank definition"; exit 1; }
done
for kw in 'collect.max_rows' '最大字节'; do
  { trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -qF "$kw" || { echo "FAIL: missing bound $kw"; exit 1; }
done
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cE '^collect\.max_rows = ' || true)" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: rules must contain exactly one collect.max_rows assignment"; exit 1; }
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cFx 'collect.max_rows = 10000' || true)" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: rules lack unique exact collect.max_rows = 10000 line"; exit 1; }
for pre in 'denoise_sql：' 'normalize_sql：' 'aggregate_sql：' 'keep_sql：'; do
  all=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cE "^(- |\* )?${pre}" || true)
  [ "$all" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: rules prefix $pre total lines $all != 1 (empty-value duplicates counted too)"; exit 1; }
  c=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cE "^(- |\* )?${pre}[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]" || true)
  [ "$c" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: rules prefix $pre value empty"; exit 1; }
done
for pre in 'normalize_sql：' 'aggregate_sql：' 'keep_sql：'; do
  frag=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk -v p="$pre" 'index($0, p)==1 || index($0, "- " p)==1 || index($0, "* " p)==1 { sub(/^(- |\* )?/, ""); sub(/^normalize_sql：[[:space:]]*/, ""); sub(/^aggregate_sql：[[:space:]]*/, ""); sub(/^keep_sql：[[:space:]]*/, ""); print; exit }')
  badf=$(printf '%s' "$frag" | tr -d "A-Za-z0-9_ .'=<>(),!%|-")
  [ -z "$badf" ] || { echo "FAIL: $pre fragment outside allowed charset: $frag"; exit 1; }
  frag_nv=$(printf '%s' "$frag" | sed "s/'[^']*'//g")
  c1=$(printf '%s' "$frag" | { grep -o '_v' || true; } | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
  c2=$(printf '%s' "$frag_nv" | { grep -o '_v' || true; } | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
  [ "$c1" = "$c2" ] || { echo "FAIL: $pre fragment must not contain _v inside quoted literals"; exit 1; }
  if [ "$pre" != 'keep_sql：' ]; then
    { trap '' PIPE; printf '%s' "$frag_nv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '{ n=split($0, tok, /[^A-Za-z0-9_]+/); for (i=1; i<=n; i++) if (tok[i]=="_v") found=1 } END{ exit !found }' || { echo "FAIL: $pre fragment must reference _v as a bare token (quoted literals do not count)"; exit 1; }
  fi
  if { trap '' PIPE; printf '%s' "$frag" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -iqE 'random|datetime|julianday|unixepoch|changes|last_insert|current_timestamp|current_date|current_time|date\(|time\('; then echo "FAIL: $pre fragment must be deterministic: $frag"; exit 1; fi
  for fn in $({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s' "$frag" 2>/dev/null || true; } | sed "s/'[^']*'//g" | grep -oE '[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*[[:space:]]*\(' | sed 's/[[:space:]]*($//; s/($//' | tr -d '(' | sort -u); do
    case "$fn" in length|ifnull|coalesce|trim|lower|upper|abs|cast|IN|in|EXISTS|exists) : ;; *) echo "FAIL: $pre function not in whitelist (length/ifnull/coalesce/trim/lower/upper/abs/cast): $fn"; exit 1 ;; esac
  done
done
nzv=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^(- |\* )?normalize_sql：[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]/{ sub(/^(- |\* )?normalize_sql：[[:space:]]*/, ""); print; exit }')
agv=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^(- |\* )?aggregate_sql：[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]/{ sub(/^(- |\* )?aggregate_sql：[[:space:]]*/, ""); print; exit }')
kpv=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^(- |\* )?keep_sql：[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]/{ sub(/^(- |\* )?keep_sql：[[:space:]]*/, ""); print; exit }')
dzv=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^(- |\* )?denoise_sql：[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]/{ sub(/^(- |\* )?denoise_sql：[[:space:]]*/, ""); print; exit }')
[ "$dzv" = "DISTINCT" ] || { echo "FAIL: denoise_sql drifted from frozen value DISTINCT: $dzv"; exit 1; }
[ "$nzv" = "trim(_v)" ] || { echo "FAIL: normalize_sql drifted from frozen value trim(_v): $nzv"; exit 1; }
[ "$agv" = "_v" ] || { echo "FAIL: aggregate_sql drifted from frozen value _v: $agv"; exit 1; }
[ "$kpv" = "repo_id = (SELECT value FROM config_kv WHERE key = 'libra.repoid' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1)" ] || { echo "FAIL: keep_sql drifted from frozen repo-scope value (R62 写死；repo_id = repo_id 类合法 SQL 形态的改写同样拒绝): $kpv"; exit 1; }
echo "OK: rules well-formed"
```

### A.8 `a8_evidence_guard.sh`（单来源证据块守卫；DC-05 / DC-10 用）

参数：`$1` = 来源名（`agent_session` 或 `operation v2`）；可选 `$2` = 目标文件（默认 `docs/development/plan/issues/468.md`）。核验：解析在全文级围栏感知视图（先对全文剔除 fenced code block——``` 与 ~~~ 两种形态、含缩进围栏（开/闭均至多 3 空格缩进，4+ 空格缩进按 CommonMark 属缩进代码块、不是围栏；反引号开围栏的信息串含反引号时整行不算围栏），按开启字符与长度配对闭合（结束围栏须同字符、长度 ≥ 开启、同样至多 3 空格缩进且无尾随非空白，不配对的围栏行视为内容）——剔除处注入非管道边界行 `##FENCE##` 防跨围栏拼接，再定位节/子节/表）上进行（数值行/标记行/命令区均须为围栏外纯文本行；围栏内展示内容不参与核验）；`## 价值门证据` heading 唯一；`### <$1> 证据块` 子节存在，含「fixture 构造」「只读」「输出摘要」标记与数值行 `成功行数：<N>`、`分母：本仓库真实 <N> 行`、`契约违规：<N>`（全量聚合值，覆盖全部谓词通过行、不受窗口截断影响）、`实际总数：<N>`——四个数值项各须**整行精确且唯一**（行首到行尾恰为约定形态，拒绝「预计成功行数：」类前缀冒充与重复行）；覆盖率行在分母 > 0 时为 `覆盖率：<0..100>%` 且等于 round(100 × 成功 ÷ 分母)、成功行数 ≤ 分母，分母 = 0 时成功行数必须为 0 且覆盖率整行恰为 `覆盖率：N/A（0/0）`（块内不得再有数值覆盖率行）；窗口标志行唯一且整行恰为 `window_truncated=true` 或 `window_truncated=false`（拒绝 `=true1` 类截断冒充）；`true` 时实际总数 > 10,000 且分母恰为 10,000，`false` 时实际总数 ≤ 10,000 且分母恰为实际总数；契约违规 ≤ 实际总数（全量聚合不得超总体）；子节含「窗口」标记与整行精确的互斥标注（`样本充足` 或 `真实样本不足 → DC-07 须判 INCONCLUSIVE`，各为整行恰等、唯一、与分母 <30 一致）。

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
src="${1:?usage: a8_evidence_guard.sh <source name> [file]}"
f="${2:-docs/development/plan/issues/468.md}"
[ -f "$f" ] || { echo "FAIL: plan file missing"; exit 2; }
fv=$(bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a12_fence_view.sh" "$f")
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cFx '## 价值门证据')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: evidence section missing"; exit 1; }
sec=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^## 价值门证据$/{ in_s=1; next } /^## / && in_s { exit } in_s')
block=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$sec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk -v h="### ${src} 证据块" '$0 == h { f=1; next } /^### / && f { exit } f')
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$sec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cFx "### ${src} 证据块")" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src evidence heading not exactly once"; exit 1; }
[ -n "$block" ] || { echo "FAIL: missing block $src"; exit 1; }
for kw in 'fixture 构造' '只读' '输出摘要'; do
  { trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -q "$kw" || { echo "FAIL: $src lacks $kw"; exit 1; }
done
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cE '^测量命令摘要：[0-9a-f]{64}$' || true)" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 缺唯一的测量命令标记行"; exit 1; }
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -c '^快照 SHA-256：' || true)" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 快照 SHA-256 行数 != 1（冲突/畸形行不得被忽略）"; exit 1; }
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cE '^快照 SHA-256：[0-9a-f]{64}$' || true)" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 快照 SHA-256 登记行形态非法（须整行恰为 快照 SHA-256：<64 hex>）"; exit 1; }
# R78-P2-1：证据块改为登记 A.13 输出的确定性摘（`测量命令摘要：<64 hex>`），A.8 以 `shasum -a 256` 重生成比对（无需把 15.7MB 大 SQL 逐字嵌入文档，避免 diff 无法评审）
cmdsum=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | sed -nE 's/^测量命令摘要：([0-9a-f]{64})$/\1/p')
[ -n "$cmdsum" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 证据块缺整行为 测量命令摘要：<64 hex> 的摘要登记行"; exit 1; }
gen=$(bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a13_measure_gen.sh" "$src" "$f")
gotsum=$(printf '%s' "$gen" | shasum -a 256 | cut -d' ' -f1)
[ "$cmdsum" = "$gotsum" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 测量命令摘要 $cmdsum != A.13 重生成摘要 $gotsum"; exit 1; }
for pre in '成功行数：' '分母：本仓库真实 ' '契约违规：' '实际总数：'; do
  [ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -c "^${pre}")" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 前缀「${pre}」行数 != 1（冲突/畸形行不得被忽略）"; exit 1; }
done
ok=$(printf '%s\n' "$block" | sed -nE 's/^成功行数：([0-9]+)$/\1/p')
den=$(printf '%s\n' "$block" | sed -nE 's/^分母：本仓库真实 ([0-9]+) 行$/\1/p')
vio=$(printf '%s\n' "$block" | sed -nE 's/^契约违规：([0-9]+)$/\1/p')
for v in "$ok" "$den" "$vio"; do
  case "$v" in *[!0-9]*) echo "FAIL: $src 非数字: $v"; exit 1 ;; esac
  case "$v" in 0?*) echo "FAIL: $src 前导零（Bash 会当八进制）: $v"; exit 1 ;; esac
done
[ "$(printf '%s\n' "$ok" | sed '/^$/d' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 成功行数行缺失或不唯一（须整行恰为 成功行数：<N>）"; exit 1; }
[ "$(printf '%s\n' "$den" | sed '/^$/d' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 分母行缺失或不唯一（须整行恰为 分母：本仓库真实 <N> 行）"; exit 1; }
[ "$(printf '%s\n' "$vio" | sed '/^$/d' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 契约违规行缺失或不唯一（须整行恰为 契约违规：<N>）"; exit 1; }
[ "$ok" -le "$den" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 成功行数 $ok > 分母 $den"; exit 1; }
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -c '^实例化事件数：' || true)" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 实例化事件数行数 != 1（冲突/畸形行不得被忽略）"; exit 1; }
nev=$(printf '%s\n' "$block" | sed -nE 's/^实例化事件数：([0-9]+)$/\1/p')
case "$nev" in ''|*[!0-9]*|0?*) echo "FAIL: $src 实例化事件数形态: $nev"; exit 1 ;; esac
[ "$nev" -le "$ok" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 实例化事件数 $nev > 成功行数 $ok"; exit 1; }
[ "$ok" -eq 0 ] || [ "$nev" -ge 1 ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 成功行数 $ok > 0 但实例化事件数 $nev = 0（每个成功行至少映射一个事件；R69-P2-3）"; exit 1; }
cov_lines=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -c '^覆盖率：' || true)
[ "$cov_lines" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 覆盖率行数 $cov_lines != 1"; exit 1; }
cov_line=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep '^覆盖率：' || true)
if [ "$den" -gt 0 ]; then
  cov=$(printf '%s' "$cov_line" | sed -n 's/^覆盖率：\([0-9]\{1,3\}\)%$/\1/p')
  [ -n "$cov" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 覆盖率行非数值形态: $cov_line"; exit 1; }
  calc=$(( (100 * ok + den / 2) / den ))
  [ "$calc" = "$cov" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 覆盖率 $cov != round(100*$ok/$den)=$calc"; exit 1; }
else
  [ "$ok" = "0" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 零分母但成功行数 $ok != 0"; exit 1; }
  [ "$cov_line" = '覆盖率：N/A（0/0）' ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 零分母覆盖率行不恰为 N/A（0/0）: $cov_line"; exit 1; }
fi
wcnt=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -c 'window_truncated=' || true)
[ "$wcnt" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src window_truncated 行数 $wcnt != 1"; exit 1; }
wline=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep 'window_truncated=' || true)
case "$wline" in
  window_truncated=true) flag=true ;;
  window_truncated=false) flag=false ;;
  *) echo "FAIL: $src 窗口行非精确形态（须整行恰为 window_truncated=true 或 window_truncated=false）: $wline"; exit 1 ;;
esac
total=$(printf '%s\n' "$block" | sed -nE 's/^实际总数：([0-9]+)$/\1/p')
[ "$(printf '%s\n' "$total" | sed '/^$/d' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 实际总数行缺失或不唯一（须整行恰为 实际总数：<N>）"; exit 1; }
case "$total" in *[!0-9]*) echo "FAIL: $src 实际总数非数字: $total"; exit 1 ;; esac
case "$total" in 0?*) echo "FAIL: $src 实际总数前导零: $total"; exit 1 ;; esac
if [ "$flag" = "true" ]; then
  [ "$total" -gt 10000 ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 截断但实际总数 $total <= 10000"; exit 1; }
  [ "$den" -eq 10000 ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 截断窗口分母 $den != 10000"; exit 1; }
else
  [ "$total" -le 10000 ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 未截断但实际总数 $total > 10000"; exit 1; }
  [ "$den" -eq "$total" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 未截断但分母 $den != 实际总数 $total"; exit 1; }
fi
[ "$vio" -le "$total" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 契约违规 $vio > 实际总数 $total"; exit 1; }
[ "$((ok + vio))" -ge "$den" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 成功行数 $ok + 契约违规 $vio = $((ok + vio)) < 分母 ${den}（谓词通过行须被成功/违规覆盖；R69-P1-6 含截断窗口）"; exit 1; }
if [ "$flag" = "false" ]; then
  [ "$((ok + vio))" -eq "$den" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 未截断时成功行数 $ok + 契约违规 $vio = $((ok + vio)) != 分母 ${den}（窗口未截断时谓词通过行被成功/违规完全划分；R68-P1-7）"; exit 1; }
fi
if [ "$den" -lt 30 ]; then
  [ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cFx '真实样本不足 → DC-07 须判 INCONCLUSIVE')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 分母 $den < 30 但缺整行恰为「真实样本不足 → DC-07 须判 INCONCLUSIVE」的标注"; exit 1; }
  [ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cFx '样本充足')" = "0" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 分母 $den < 30 但含整行「样本充足」"; exit 1; }
else
  [ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cFx '样本充足')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 分母 $den >= 30 但缺整行恰为「样本充足」的标注"; exit 1; }
  [ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cFx '真实样本不足 → DC-07 须判 INCONCLUSIVE')" = "0" ] || { echo "FAIL: $src 分母 $den >= 30 但含整行样本不足标注"; exit 1; }
fi
{ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -q '窗口' || { echo "FAIL: $src lacks 窗口 policy"; exit 1; }
echo "OK: $src evidence block well-formed"
```

### A.9 `a9_verdict_guard.sh`（价值门结论节守卫；DC-07 用）

可选参数 `$1` = 目标文件（默认 `docs/development/plan/issues/468.md`）。核验：父标题、结论 token 与时间箱行在全文级围栏感知视图（先对全文剔除 fenced code block——``` 与 ~~~ 两种形态、含缩进围栏（开/闭均至多 3 空格缩进，4+ 空格缩进按 CommonMark 属缩进代码块、不是围栏；反引号开围栏的信息串含反引号时整行不算围栏），按开启字符与长度配对闭合（结束围栏须同字符、长度 ≥ 开启、同样至多 3 空格缩进且无尾随非空白，不配对的围栏行视为内容）——剔除处注入非管道边界行 `##FENCE##` 防跨围栏拼接，再定位节/子节/表）上核验（围栏内的示例结论/示例时间行不计）；判据块逐字比对在保留围栏内容的节级保真副本上进行（其本身是计划内规范判据围栏，`A.9` 为全文唯一读取真实围栏内容的守卫；两侧提取结果经 `mktemp` 落盘后 `cmp` 逐字节比较——R66-P2-1：命令替换会剥掉尾部换行，`x\n` 与 `x\n\n` 曾被误判一致）；判据块内容以配对式扫描器提取完整内容（不按三反引号行计数，四反引号包裹内的伪判据段不得截断冒充）；卡侧取「判据（写死」标记行之后的首个围栏（标记前的示例围栏不得冒充），结论节侧要求节内恰含一个围栏并取其内容（多/少均失败）；`## 价值门结论` heading 唯一；首个非空行的首个空白分隔 token 恰为 `GO` / `NO-GO` / `INCONCLUSIVE` 之一；节内含**唯一**「起止：」时间行（两个日期戳；重复行即失败，防止先写短时长示例行绕过超期判定），起 ≤ 止且时长 ≤ 43200 秒——**时长超 43200 秒（时间箱超期）时，唯一可通过的形态是结论恰为 `INCONCLUSIVE`**（与 DC-07 判定顺序 ① 及故障恢复矩阵的超时分支一致；超期记录不得携带 `GO` / `NO-GO`）；节内的判据段（首个 fenced code block 内容）与 DC-07 卡 Description 中「判据（写死」标记后的首个 fenced code block 内容逐字一致；判据段含「技术可行性」「达标」「分子」「分母」「具名消费者」「判定顺序」「窗口截断」字面。

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
f="${1:-docs/development/plan/issues/468.md}"
[ -f "$f" ] || { echo "FAIL: plan file missing"; exit 2; }
fv=$(bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a12_fence_view.sh" "$f")
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cFx '## 价值门结论')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: verdict section missing"; exit 1; }
sec=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^## 价值门结论$/{ in_s=1; next } /^## / && in_s { exit } in_s')
raw_sec=$(awk '
  function fencelen(   s, nsp, ch, n, info) {
    s = $0
    nsp = 0
    while (substr(s, nsp + 1, 1) == " ") nsp++
    if (nsp > 3) return 0
    s = substr(s, nsp + 1)
    ch = substr(s, 1, 1)
    if (ch != "`" && ch != "~") return 0
    n = 0
    while (substr(s, n + 1, 1) == ch) n++
    if (n < 3) return 0
    if (ch == "`") {
      info = substr(s, n + 1)
      if (info ~ /`/) return 0
    }
    fch = ch
    return n
  }
  function fenceclose(   s, nsp, ch, n, rest) {
    s = $0
    nsp = 0
    while (substr(s, nsp + 1, 1) == " ") nsp++
    if (nsp > 3) return 0
    s = substr(s, nsp + 1)
    ch = substr(s, 1, 1)
    if (ch != fch) return 0
    n = 0
    while (substr(s, n + 1, 1) == ch) n++
    if (n < flen) return 0
    rest = substr(s, n + 1)
    return (rest ~ /^[ \t]*$/)
  }
  fenc {
    if (in_s) print
    if (fenceclose()) fenc = 0
    next
  }
  {
    flen = fencelen()
    if (flen) { fenc = 1; if (in_s) print; next }
    if (!in_s && /^## 价值门结论$/) { in_s = 1; next }
    if (in_s && /^## /) exit
    if (in_s) print
  }
' "$f")
first=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$sec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk 'NF { print; exit }')
token=${first%%[[:space:]]*}
case "$token" in
  GO|NO-GO|INCONCLUSIVE) : ;;
  *) echo "FAIL: first token is not a verdict: $token"; exit 1 ;;
esac
tb=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s' "$sec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep '起止：' || true)
[ "$(printf '%s\n' "$tb" | sed '/^$/d' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: timebox line missing or not unique"; exit 1; }
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s' "$tb" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -o '起止：' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: timebox line must contain exactly one 起止： marker"; exit 1; }
{ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$tb" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -qE '^起止：[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2} [0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2} UTC [–—-] [0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2} [0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2} UTC$'   || { echo "FAIL: timebox line must be exactly 起止：<ts> UTC <sep> <ts> UTC and nothing else (no timezone offsets, no trailing content)"; exit 1; }
ts=$(printf '%s' "$tb" | { grep -oE '[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2} [0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}' || true; })
[ "$(printf '%s\n' "$ts" | sed '/^$/d' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')" = "2" ] || { echo "FAIL: timebox line must contain exactly two timestamps (no more, no fewer)"; exit 1; }
t1=$(printf '%s\n' "$ts" | sed -n '1p')
t2=$(printf '%s\n' "$ts" | sed -n '2p')
if date --version >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  e1=$(date -u -d "$t1" +%s); e2=$(date -u -d "$t2" +%s)
else
  e1=$(date -u -j -f '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S' "$t1" +%s); e2=$(date -u -j -f '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S' "$t2" +%s)
fi
[ "$e2" -ge "$e1" ] || { echo "FAIL: timebox end before start"; exit 1; }
if [ $((e2 - e1)) -gt 43200 ]; then
  [ "$token" = "INCONCLUSIVE" ] || { echo "FAIL: timebox exceeded but verdict is $token (must be INCONCLUSIVE)"; exit 1; }
fi
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -c "^### Task DC-07：" || true)" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: DC-07 card heading not exactly once"; exit 1; }
card=$(awk '
  function fencelen(   s, nsp, ch, n, info) {
    s = $0
    nsp = 0
    while (substr(s, nsp + 1, 1) == " ") nsp++
    if (nsp > 3) return 0
    s = substr(s, nsp + 1)
    ch = substr(s, 1, 1)
    if (ch != "`" && ch != "~") return 0
    n = 0
    while (substr(s, n + 1, 1) == ch) n++
    if (n < 3) return 0
    if (ch == "`") {
      info = substr(s, n + 1)
      if (info ~ /`/) return 0
    }
    fch = ch
    return n
  }
  function fenceclose(   s, nsp, ch, n, rest) {
    s = $0
    nsp = 0
    while (substr(s, nsp + 1, 1) == " ") nsp++
    if (nsp > 3) return 0
    s = substr(s, nsp + 1)
    ch = substr(s, 1, 1)
    if (ch != fch) return 0
    n = 0
    while (substr(s, n + 1, 1) == ch) n++
    if (n < flen) return 0
    rest = substr(s, n + 1)
    return (rest ~ /^[ \t]*$/)
  }
  fenc {
    if (inc) print
    if (fenceclose()) fenc = 0
    next
  }
  {
    flen = fencelen()
    if (flen) { fenc = 1; if (inc) print; next }
    if (!inc && index($0, "### Task DC-07：") == 1) { inc = 1; next }
    if (inc && (/^### Task / || /^## /)) exit
    if (inc) print
  }
' "$f")
[ -n "$card" ] || { echo "FAIL: DC-07 card missing"; exit 1; }
{ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$card" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -q '判据（写死' || { echo "FAIL: DC-07 lacks 判据（写死 marker"; exit 1; }
tmpcc=$(mktemp)
tmpsc=$(mktemp)
trap 'rm -f "$tmpcc" "$tmpsc"' EXIT
{ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s' "$card" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '
  function fencelen(   s, nsp, ch, n, info) {
    s = $0
    nsp = 0
    while (substr(s, nsp + 1, 1) == " ") nsp++
    if (nsp > 3) return 0
    s = substr(s, nsp + 1)
    ch = substr(s, 1, 1)
    if (ch != "`" && ch != "~") return 0
    n = 0
    while (substr(s, n + 1, 1) == ch) n++
    if (n < 3) return 0
    if (ch == "`") {
      info = substr(s, n + 1)
      if (info ~ /`/) return 0
    }
    fch = ch
    return n
  }
  function fenceclose(   s, nsp, ch, n, rest) {
    s = $0
    nsp = 0
    while (substr(s, nsp + 1, 1) == " ") nsp++
    if (nsp > 3) return 0
    s = substr(s, nsp + 1)
    ch = substr(s, 1, 1)
    if (ch != fch) return 0
    n = 0
    while (substr(s, n + 1, 1) == ch) n++
    if (n < flen) return 0
    rest = substr(s, n + 1)
    return (rest ~ /^[ \t]*$/)
  }
  fenc { if (fenceclose()) { fenc = 0; if (mark) done = 1; next } if (mark && !done) print; next }
  !mark { flen = fencelen(); if (flen) { fenc = 1; next }; if (index($0, "判据（写死") > 0) mark = 1; next }
  done { next }
  { flen = fencelen(); if (flen) fenc = 1; next }
  END { if (fenc) { print "FAIL: card criteria fence unclosed" > "/dev/stderr"; exit 1 } }
' > "$tmpcc"
{ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s' "$raw_sec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '
  function fencelen(   s, nsp, ch, n, info) {
    s = $0
    nsp = 0
    while (substr(s, nsp + 1, 1) == " ") nsp++
    if (nsp > 3) return 0
    s = substr(s, nsp + 1)
    ch = substr(s, 1, 1)
    if (ch != "`" && ch != "~") return 0
    n = 0
    while (substr(s, n + 1, 1) == ch) n++
    if (n < 3) return 0
    if (ch == "`") {
      info = substr(s, n + 1)
      if (info ~ /`/) return 0
    }
    fch = ch
    return n
  }
  function fenceclose(   s, nsp, ch, n, rest) {
    s = $0
    nsp = 0
    while (substr(s, nsp + 1, 1) == " ") nsp++
    if (nsp > 3) return 0
    s = substr(s, nsp + 1)
    ch = substr(s, 1, 1)
    if (ch != fch) return 0
    n = 0
    while (substr(s, n + 1, 1) == ch) n++
    if (n < flen) return 0
    rest = substr(s, n + 1)
    return (rest ~ /^[ \t]*$/)
  }
  fenc {
    if (fenceclose()) { fenc = 0; if (nf == 1) done = 1 }
    else if (nf == 1 && !done) print
    next
  }
  { flen = fencelen(); if (flen) { fenc = 1; nf++ } ; next }
  END { if (nf != 1 || fenc) { print "FAIL: verdict section criteria fence not exactly once or unclosed" > "/dev/stderr"; exit 1 } }
' > "$tmpsc"
[ -s "$tmpcc" ] || { echo "FAIL: DC-07 criteria block empty"; exit 1; }
# R69-P1-4：判据块须与钉死的冻结值逐字一致（两侧可编辑副本同改阈值仍过；钉死防漂移）
pinned=$(printf '%s\n' '- 技术可行性：契约能在「无新 ingest、无新权威或投影存储、纯本地离线」三约束下被两类既有事实面实例化，且每条规则都有确定性输出定义，证据命令可重跑。' '- 达标：分子 = 实例化成功行数；覆盖率 = 分子 ÷ 分母；该来源达标 = 覆盖率 ≥ 90% 且契约违规计数 = 0（违规计数为全量聚合口径，覆盖全部谓词通过行，不受窗口截断影响）；要求两类来源均达标。达标比较以整数交叉乘法在原始数值上执行（10 × 分子 ≥ 9 × 分母），四舍五入后的展示百分比不作判定依据。' '- 具名消费者：已登记消费 collect.event.v1 的具体计划卡或 issue（引用其文件/编号）；stats.export.v1 与 episode 两面的当前状态不得充当消费者（ADR-DC-04）。' '- 判定顺序（互斥）：① 零分母、真实样本不足（证据节标注）、证据不足或时间箱超期 → INCONCLUSIVE；② 技术可行性不满足 → NO-GO；③ 两类来源均达标且存在具名消费者 → GO；④ 其余 → NO-GO。窗口截断（已登记实际总数）不阻塞判定。
- 证据时效：DC-05/DC-10 测量须取自已注册的时效内证据（判定与测量完成间隔 ≤ 24 小时）；超期先重测两来源再判定，否则判 INCONCLUSIVE（R78-P1-2）。' '- 证据时效：DC-05/DC-10 测量须取自已注册的时效内证据（判定与测量完成间隔 ≤ 24 小时）；超期先重测两来源再判定，否则判 INCONCLUSIVE（R78-P1-2）。
[ "$(cat "$tmpcc")" = "$pinned" ] || { echo "FAIL: criteria block drifted from pinned frozen value (R69-P1-4；两侧可编辑副本同改阈值也不再通过)"; exit 1; }
cmp -s "$tmpcc" "$tmpsc" || { echo "FAIL: criteria block differs from DC-07 card (byte-exact comparison via temp files; command substitution would strip trailing newlines, R66-P2-1)"; exit 1; }
for kw in '技术可行性' '达标' '分子' '分母' '具名消费者' '判定顺序' '窗口截断' '覆盖率 ≥ 90%' '且契约违规计数 = 0' '10 × 分子 ≥ 9 × 分母' '无新 ingest、无新权威或投影存储、纯本地离线' 'INCONCLUSIVE' 'NO-GO' 'GO' '证据时效' '≤ 24 小时'; do
  grep -qF "$kw" "$tmpcc" || { echo "FAIL: criteria lacks frozen constant: $kw"; exit 1; }
done
echo "OK: verdict section well-formed ($token)"
```

### A.10 `a10_mapping_guard.sh`（下游映射说明单子节守卫；DC-09 / DC-14 用）

参数：`$1` = 子节名（`stats.export.v1` 或 `episode`）；可选 `$2` = 目标文件（默认 `docs/development/plan/issues/468.md`）。核验：`## 下游映射说明` heading 唯一；`### <$1>` 子节在节内存在且恰一次（解析在全文级围栏感知视图（先对全文剔除 fenced code block——``` 与 ~~~ 两种形态、含缩进围栏（开/闭均至多 3 空格缩进，4+ 空格缩进按 CommonMark 属缩进代码块、不是围栏；反引号开围栏的信息串含反引号时整行不算围栏），按开启字符与长度配对闭合（结束围栏须同字符、长度 ≥ 开启、同样至多 3 空格缩进且无尾随非空白，不配对的围栏行视为内容）——剔除处注入非管道边界行 `##FENCE##` 防跨围栏拼接，再定位节/子节/表）上进行；围栏内的示例标题/表格/清单一律不计），含字段级对照表（表头逐字为 `| collect.event.v1 字段 | 下游字段 | 缺口 |` 且在子节内唯一，表头/分隔行/数据行连续成表——首个非管道行即终止解析；分隔行四竖线且逐格至少三个连字符、至少一行数据行紧随分隔行；数据行逐行恰好四个竖线、首列非空、且「下游字段」与「缺口」两列至少一项非空）与子节内的缺口清单（标记行整行恰为 `缺口清单：` 且在子节内唯一；其边界——下一标题或表格止，缩进形态亦终止——内至少一条、至多十二条（与字典字段数 + 信封字段数同界）以 `- ` 起头且逐条非空的清单项，`* ` 项不算；对照表缺口列非空的每个单元格，其首列字段须在清单项中以完整 token 形态被提及——只检索 `- ` 清单项、切词后整词比对、不做子串匹配，逐格核验）；**字段覆盖：** 「契约草案」节 `### 字段字典` 小节首表（首行表头逐字核验）的全部字段名须在该子节对照表首列恰好出现一次（缺字段、重复行、字典外幻影字段均拒绝）；**下游存在性锚点：** `stats.export.v1` 子节须含 `issues/500.md` 字面、`episode` 子节须含 `plan-20260926.md` 字面（下游字段的存在性核对锚点）。

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
sub="${1:?usage: a10_mapping_guard.sh <stats.export.v1|episode> [file]}"
case "$sub" in stats.export.v1|episode) : ;; *) echo "FAIL: unknown subsection $sub"; exit 2 ;; esac
f="${2:-docs/development/plan/issues/468.md}"
[ -f "$f" ] || { echo "FAIL: plan file missing"; exit 2; }
# R67-P1-5：映射覆盖输入依赖字典；在采用当前字典前先重验其冻结内容（否则从字典与映射中同删 `end_ts` 仍会假绿）
bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a5_dictionary_guard.sh" "$f" >&2 || { echo "FAIL: dictionary drift (A.5 re-check before mapping coverage)"; exit 1; }
fv=$(bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a12_fence_view.sh" "$f")
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cFx '## 下游映射说明')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: heading not unique"; exit 1; }
sec=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^## 下游映射说明$/{ in_s=1; next } /^## / && in_s { exit } in_s')
# 字段覆盖输入：本文件「契约草案」节 `### 字段字典` 小节的全部字段名（DC-12 产物，DC-09/DC-14 时必已存在）
csec=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^## 契约草案$/{ in_s=1; next } /^## / && in_s { exit } in_s')
dict=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s' "$csec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^### 字段字典$/{f=1;next} /^### /&&f{exit} f')
[ -n "$dict" ] || { echo "FAIL: dictionary subsection missing (needed for mapping coverage)"; exit 1; }
drows=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$dict" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^\|/ { print; d=1; next } d { exit }')
[ "$(printf '%s\n' "$drows" | sed -n '1p')" = '| 字段名 | 类型 | 必填性 | 来源事实面 | 来源锚点 | 最大字节 |' ] || { echo "FAIL: dictionary first table header mismatch (needed for mapping coverage)"; exit 1; }
fields=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$drows" 2>/dev/null || true; } | sed -n '3,$p' | awk -F'|' 'NF { n=$2; gsub(/^ +| +$/,"",n); print n }' | sort -u)
# R71-P2-4：映射还要对信封字段 `event_type` 与 `schema_version` 作明确决策（下游字段/缺口至少一项），不得跳过
fields=$(printf '%s\n' "$fields" 'event_type' 'schema_version' | sort -u)
[ -n "$fields" ] || { echo "FAIL: dictionary fields empty"; exit 1; }
block=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$sec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk -v h="### ${sub}" '$0 == h { f=1; next } /^### / && f { exit } f')
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$sec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cFx "### ${sub}")" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub heading not exactly once"; exit 1; }
[ -n "$block" ] || { echo "FAIL: missing subsection $sub"; exit 1; }
case "$sub" in
  stats.export.v1) anchor='issues/500.md' ;;
  episode) anchor='plan-20260926.md' ;;
esac
{ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -qF "$anchor" || { echo "FAIL: $sub subsection lacks downstream schema anchor $anchor"; exit 1; }
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cFx '| collect.event.v1 字段 | 下游字段 | 缺口 |')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub mapping table header not exactly once in subsection"; exit 1; }
rows=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '$0=="| collect.event.v1 字段 | 下游字段 | 缺口 |"{f=1} f && /^\|/{print} f && !/^\|/{exit}')
[ "$(printf '%s\n' "$rows" | sed '/^$/d' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')" -ge 3 ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub mapping table needs contiguous header+separator+>=1 data row"; exit 1; }
hdr=$(printf '%s\n' "$rows" | sed -n '1p')
[ "$hdr" = '| collect.event.v1 字段 | 下游字段 | 缺口 |' ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub header mismatch"; exit 1; }
sep=$(printf '%s\n' "$rows" | sed -n '2p')
case "$sep" in \|*\|) : ;; *) echo "FAIL: $sub separator row not pipe-delimited: $sep"; exit 1 ;; esac
pc=$(printf '%s' "$sep" | tr -cd '|' | wc -c | tr -d ' ')
[ "$pc" = "4" ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub separator pipe count $pc != 4"; exit 1; }
inner=${sep#\|}; inner=${inner%\|}
i=0
OLDIFS=$IFS; IFS='|'
for c in $inner; do
  c=$(printf '%s' "$c" | tr -d ' ')
  core=${c#:}; core=${core%:}
  case "$core" in *[!-]*|'') echo "FAIL: $sub separator cell not dashes: $c"; exit 1;; esac
  [ "${#core}" -ge 3 ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub separator cell too short: $c"; exit 1; }
  i=$((i+1))
done
IFS=$OLDIFS
[ "$i" -eq 3 ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub separator cells $i != 3"; exit 1; }
dat=$(printf '%s\n' "$rows" | sed -n '3,$p')
printf '%s\n' "$dat" | while IFS= read -r d; do
  [ -z "$d" ] && continue
  p=$(printf '%s' "$d" | tr -cd '|' | wc -c | tr -d ' ')
  [ "$p" = "4" ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub data row not exactly 3 columns: $d"; exit 1; }
  v=$(printf '%s' "$d" | cut -d'|' -f2 | sed 's/^ *//;s/ *$//')
  [ -n "$v" ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub data row empty first cell"; exit 1; }
  down=$(printf '%s' "$d" | cut -d'|' -f3 | sed 's/^ *//;s/ *$//')
  gap=$(printf '%s' "$d" | cut -d'|' -f4 | sed 's/^ *//;s/ *$//')
  if [ -z "$down" ] && [ -z "$gap" ]; then echo "FAIL: $sub data row has neither downstream field nor gap: $d"; exit 1; fi
done
mapf=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$dat" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk -F'|' 'NF { n=$2; gsub(/^ +| +$/,"",n); print n }')
dup=$(printf '%s\n' "$mapf" | sed '/^$/d' | sort | uniq -d)
[ -z "$dup" ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub mapping has duplicate field rows: $dup"; exit 1; }
miss=$(comm -23 <(printf '%s\n' "$fields") <(printf '%s\n' "$mapf" | sort -u))
[ -z "$miss" ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub mapping does not cover dictionary fields:"; printf '%s\n' "$miss"; exit 1; }
extra=$(comm -13 <(printf '%s\n' "$fields") <(printf '%s\n' "$mapf" | sort -u))
[ -z "$extra" ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub mapping has non-dictionary fields:"; printf '%s\n' "$extra"; exit 1; }
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cFx '缺口清单：')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub gap-list marker line not exactly once (exact: 缺口清单：)"; exit 1; }
gl=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^缺口清单：$/{f=1;next} f && (/^[[:space:]]*#/ || /^[[:space:]]*\|/){exit} f')
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$gl" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -c '^- ')" -ge 1 ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub gap list has no '- ' entry within its boundary (next heading or table ends it)"; exit 1; }
glc=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$gl" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -c '^- ')
[ "$glc" -le 12 ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub gap list item count $glc exceeds bound 12 (dictionary fields + envelope fields; R73-P1-7)"; exit 1; }
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$gl" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^- /{ sub(/^- /,""); if ($0 ~ /[^[:space:]]/) n++ } END{ print n+0 }')" = "$glc" ] || { echo "FAIL: $sub gap list has empty '- ' entry"; exit 1; }
items=$(printf '%s\n' "$gl" | sed -n 's/^- //p')
printf '%s\n' "$dat" | while IFS= read -r d; do
  if [ -n "$d" ]; then
    fld=$(printf '%s' "$d" | cut -d'|' -f2 | sed 's/^ *//;s/ *$//')
    gp=$(printf '%s' "$d" | cut -d'|' -f4 | sed 's/^ *//;s/ *$//')
    if [ -n "$gp" ]; then
      if ! { trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$items" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk -v f="$fld" '{ n=split($0, tok, /[^A-Za-z0-9_.]+/); for (i=1; i<=n; i++) if (tok[i]==f) found=1 } END{ exit !found }'; then
        echo "FAIL: $sub gap cell of field $fld not reflected (whole-token, items only) in gap list"; exit 1
      fi
    fi
  fi
done
echo "OK: mapping subsection $sub well-formed"
```

### A.11 `a11_closeout_guard.sh`（收口核验；DC-04 用）

可选参数 `$1` = 目标文件（默认 `docs/development/plan/issues/468.md`）。核验：解析在全文级围栏感知视图（先对全文剔除 fenced code block——``` 与 ~~~ 两种形态、含缩进围栏（开/闭均至多 3 空格缩进，4+ 空格缩进按 CommonMark 属缩进代码块、不是围栏；反引号开围栏的信息串含反引号时整行不算围栏），按开启字符与长度配对闭合（结束围栏须同字符、长度 ≥ 开启、同样至多 3 空格缩进且无尾随非空白，不配对的围栏行视为内容）——剔除处注入非管道边界行 `##FENCE##` 防跨围栏拼接，再定位节/子节/表）上进行（围栏内的示例行不计）；「价值门结论」节首行 token 恰为三态之一；「非目标与延后项」表存在 `| DEFER-DC-01 |` 行且行内含 `DC-07`、`GO`、`另立实现计划` 字面；「修订历史」存在匹配 `DC-04 收口执行@[0-9a-f]{7,}` 的行（含收口门被测树标识形态，旧修订行不匹配）。

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
f="${1:-docs/development/plan/issues/468.md}"
[ -f "$f" ] || { echo "FAIL: plan file missing"; exit 2; }
fv=$(bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a12_fence_view.sh" "$f")
sec=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^## 价值门结论$/{ in_s=1; next } /^## / && in_s { exit } in_s')
[ -n "$sec" ] || { echo "FAIL: verdict section missing"; exit 1; }
first=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$sec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk 'NF { print; exit }')
token=${first%%[[:space:]]*}
case "$token" in
  GO|NO-GO|INCONCLUSIVE) : ;;
  *) echo "FAIL: verdict token: $token"; exit 1 ;;
esac
dsec=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^## 非目标与延后项$/{ in_s=1; next } /^## / && in_s { exit } in_s')
[ -n "$dsec" ] || { echo "FAIL: 非目标与延后项 section missing"; exit 1; }
# R68-P2-1：先按表头锁定连续表（表头/分隔行/数据行连续、首个非管道行即止），再在表内选 DEFER 行；R69-P2-4：校验表头后紧跟 Markdown 分隔行，缺分隔行即拒（无分隔行时数据行直接紧随表头）
 # R71-P2-1：允许 Markdown 表缩进（行首空白）——表头/数据行匹配时先剥行首空白
 defer=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$dsec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | sed 's/^ \{0,3\}//' | awk '$0=="| ID | 延后内容 | 原因 | 重启条件 | 承接位置 |"{f=1} f && /^\|/{print} f && !/^\|/{exit}')
 dtab=$defer
 # R70-P2-3：整个「非目标与延后项」节内 DEFER 表头与 DEFER-DC-01 行都只可出现一次（第二张旁表汇入冲突行即拒）；R71-P2-1：先按行首空白归一（表头/行匹配容忍缩进）
 [ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$dsec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | sed 's/^ \{0,3\}//' | grep -cFx '| ID | 延后内容 | 原因 | 重启条件 | 承接位置 |' || true)" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: DEFER table header not exactly once in 非目标与延后项 section (R70-P2-3/R71-P2-1 容忍缩进)"; exit 1; }
 [ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$dsec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | sed 's/^ \{0,3\}//' | awk -F'|' '$0 ~ /^\|/ && $2 ~ /^ *DEFER-DC-01 *$/' | sed '/^$/d' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: DEFER-DC-01 row not exactly once across 非目标与延后项 section (R71-P2-1/R72-P2-1，先剥行首空白容忍缩进)"; exit 1; }
 [ "$(printf '%s\n' "$dtab" | sed -n '1p')" = '| ID | 延后内容 | 原因 | 重启条件 | 承接位置 |' ] || { echo "FAIL: DEFER table header missing"; exit 1; }
 [ "$(printf '%s\n' "$dtab" | sed -n '2p')" = '| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |' ] || { echo "FAIL: DEFER table separator missing/not immediately after header (R69-P2-4)"; exit 1; }
 defer=$(printf '%s\n' "$dtab" | sed -n '3,$p' | awk -F'|' '$2 ~ /^ *DEFER-DC-01 *$/')
[ "$(printf '%s\n' "$defer" | sed '/^$/d' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: DEFER-DC-01 row not exactly once in 非目标与延后项 table (prose citation containing | DEFER-DC-01 | does not count; R67-P1-6)"; exit 1; }
restart=$(printf '%s\n' "$defer" | awk -F'|' 'NF { r=$5; gsub(/^ +| +$/,"",r); print r }')
printf '%s' "$restart" | grep -qF 'DC-07 判 `GO`' || { echo "FAIL: DEFER-DC-01 restart condition must contain the single contiguous 'DC-07 判 `GO`' clause (R72-P1-8；`DC-07 判 以后再议；管理者 GO` 不行): '$restart'"; exit 1; }
printf '%s' "$restart" | grep -qF '且另立实现计划' || { echo "FAIL: DEFER-DC-01 restart condition lacks '且另立实现计划' (R68-P1-8): '$restart'"; exit 1; }
printf '%s' "$restart" | grep -qE '(^|[^A-Za-z0-9_])GO([^A-Za-z0-9_]|$)' || { echo "FAIL: DEFER-DC-01 restart condition must gate on a standalone GO (R68-P1-8; 'NO-GO or GO' 形式不过): '$restart'"; exit 1; }
if printf '%s' "$restart" | grep -q 'NO-GO' || printf '%s' "$restart" | grep -q 'INCONCLUSIVE' || printf '%s' "$restart" | grep -q '或' || printf '%s' "$restart" | grep -q '待定'; then echo "FAIL: DEFER-DC-01 restart condition must allow only a single GO restart branch (no NO-GO/INCONCLUSIVE/或/待定; R69-P1-5/R71-P1-10): '$restart'"; exit 1; fi
[ "$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -cFx '### 修订历史' || true)" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: 修订历史 heading not exactly once"; exit 1; }
{ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^### 修订历史$/{ f=1; next } f && (/^## / || /^### /) { exit } f' \
  | grep -Eq '^\|.*DC-04 收口执行@[0-9a-f]{7,}.*\|' \
  || { echo "FAIL: no 'DC-04 收口执行@<sha>' revision table row"; exit 1; }
# R73-P2-2：把收口行标记与「本卡提交前 HEAD」（DC-04 的测试父基线）绑定——注册值若存在则须匹配（否则旧行冒充）；此处仅登记形态与一致性，不在收口时点核对具体 SHA
# R73-P2-2/R76-P1-2：收口标记是「被测树」记录；收口门本卡提交前运行、被测父为当时 HEAD，但 A.11 会在 pre-registration 阶段（后续提交之后）复跑——故用「记录 SHA 是当前 HEAD 的祖先」判定（两阶段均可通过），并解析**恰一个完整 7–40-hex** token（从 `DC-04 收口执行@<sha>` 订阅行整行取，剥离非 hex 尾部）
closerec=$(printf '%s\n' "$fv" | awk '/^### 修订历史$/{s=1;next} /^## /&&s{exit} /^### /&&s{exit} s' | grep -F 'DC-04 收口执行@' | tail -1 | sed 's/.*DC-04 收口执行@//' | awk '{print $1}')
if [ -n "$closerec" ]; then
  case "$closerec" in [0-9a-f]*) : ;; *) echo "FAIL: DC-04 收口执行@$closerec not hex (R73-P2-2)"; exit 1 ;; esac
  fullsha=$(libra rev-parse "$closerec") || { echo "FAIL: DC-04 收口执行@$closerec not a commit (R73-P2-2)"; exit 1; }
  libra merge-base --is-ancestor "$fullsha" HEAD || { echo "FAIL: DC-04 收口执行@$closerec ($fullsha) is not an ancestor of current HEAD (R76-P1-2 被测树必须是祖先，两阶段复验均通过)"; exit 1; }
fi
echo "OK: closeout consistent ($token)"
```

### A.12 `a12_fence_view.sh`（全文围栏感知视图助手；非门禁，被各门复用）

参数：`$1` = 目标文件（必填）。输出：剔除 fenced code block 后的全文视图（``` 与 ~~~ 两种形态；开/闭均至多 3 空格缩进（4+ 空格缩进属缩进代码块、不是围栏）；反引号开围栏的信息串含反引号时整行不算围栏；按开启字符与长度配对闭合——结束围栏须同字符、长度 ≥ 开启、尾随只允许空白，不配对的围栏行视为内容；剔除处注入非管道边界行 `##FENCE##` 防跨围栏拼接）。A.1/A.3–A.11 与卡内零命中守卫、终态状态门均以 `fv=$(bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a12_fence_view.sh" <file>)` 取得视图。

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
f="${1:?usage: a12_fence_view.sh <file>}"
[ -f "$f" ] || { echo "FAIL: plan file missing"; exit 2; }
awk '
  function fencelen(   s, nsp, ch, n, info) {
    s = $0
    nsp = 0
    while (substr(s, nsp + 1, 1) == " ") nsp++
    if (nsp > 3) return 0
    s = substr(s, nsp + 1)
    ch = substr(s, 1, 1)
    if (ch != "`" && ch != "~") return 0
    n = 0
    while (substr(s, n + 1, 1) == ch) n++
    if (n < 3) return 0
    if (ch == "`") {
      info = substr(s, n + 1)
      if (info ~ /`/) return 0
    }
    fch = ch
    return n
  }
  function fenceclose(   s, nsp, ch, n, rest) {
    s = $0
    nsp = 0
    while (substr(s, nsp + 1, 1) == " ") nsp++
    if (nsp > 3) return 0
    s = substr(s, nsp + 1)
    ch = substr(s, 1, 1)
    if (ch != fch) return 0
    n = 0
    while (substr(s, n + 1, 1) == ch) n++
    if (n < flen) return 0
    rest = substr(s, n + 1)
    return (rest ~ /^[ \t]*$/)
  }
  !fenc {
    flen = fencelen()
    if (flen) { fenc = 1; print "##FENCE##"; next }
    print
    next
  }
  {
    if (fenceclose()) fenc = 0
    next
  }
' "$f"
```

### A.13 `a13_measure_gen.sh`（测量命令生成器；非门禁，DC-05/DC-10 消费，A.8 重生成比对）

参数：`$1` = 来源名（`agent_session` 或 `operation v2`）；可选 `$2` = 目标文件。输出：该来源的测量 SQL（纯文本五行，固定顺序：谓词通过行总数 / 违规全量聚合 / 实例化事件数（窗口内 + `NOT (<违规>)` + `keep_sql` 保留谓词过滤 + 归一/聚合真实执行：归一片段为含 `_v` 占位的标量表达式、逐字段代入生成规范化值；事件文本编码为长度前缀 + `∅` NULL 标记的无碰撞形态（`length:value` 逐字段拼接）；去噪 = 对事件文本 `DISTINCT` 折叠，聚合 = 对 `aggregate_sql[_v := 事件文本]` 键再折叠；事件类型字面入投影）/ 映射成功输入行数（未去重：窗口内 + `NOT (<违规>)` + `keep_sql` 保留谓词过滤，覆盖率分子）/ keyset 游标页（`__CURSOR__` 与 `__PAGELIMIT__` 占位、仅判长投影不读原值）），SQL 只作数据经 argv 直传 sqlite3（不经 shell 再解析），由「契约草案」节定稿内容确定性生成：表名与 WHERE 片段取自谓词子块的 `table：`/`where_sql：` 机器可读行（table 须为裸标识符）；窗口上限取自规则小节整行 `collect.max_rows = <N>`；违规析取按字典逐字段生成（必填 → `IS NULL`；全部字段 → 字节上限双重判定：原值 `length(CAST(<f> AS BLOB)) > <类型上限>` 即违规（超限原值不进入归一化与任何投影计算），且归一化值 `length(CAST((<nzf>) AS BLOB)) > <类型上限>` 亦违规；枚举/计数/布尔形态断言作用于归一化值。**R61 增两条：**① 归一化值为 NULL 的语义——必填字段 → 该行计契约违规（`(<nzf>) IS NULL` 析取；归一化不得抹除必填值），可选字段 → 合法（事件编码 `∅` 标记承载）；全部归一化值断言以 `IS NOT NULL` 守卫，违规谓词为总函数（永真或永假，无 SQL 三值逻辑 NULL 传播把该行从违规与成功两侧同时静默漏计）。② 枚举字段须在「枚举值域」小节有恰一条值域行且其值非空——先统计该字段全部值域行（表头与分隔行不计）要求恰一行、再校验值非空，缺失/重复/空值即非零退出（生成器 fail-closed；不得在值域缺失时静默放弃 `NOT IN` 校验，也不得经删空行后计数放行「有效行 + 空值行」组合）。**R62 增三条：**③ 生成前先以 A.5（字典/值域）+ A.6（本来源谓词）+ A.7（精炼规则）重验——消费内容若在前置卡后漂移（如被注入 `random()`），非零退出（守卫诊断走 stderr，stdout 恰为五行 SQL）。④ 片段标识符闭集校验前先剔除单引号字面值与冻结的 repo 身份子查询 `(SELECT value FROM config_kv WHERE key = 'libra.repoid' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1)`（锚定 `src/internal/workspace.rs:1864-1865` 的 `REPO_IDENTITY_SUBQUERY`——repo 归属收口于 `keep_sql` 的合法形态，剔除后再逐 token 校验）。⑤ A.8 重生成比对与 A.14 测量均经本生成器，上述守卫对所有消费路径一致生效。**R63 增一条：**⑥ A.5/A.6/A.7 已对消费内容做逐字冻结核验（字典整块、值域整块、`table：`/`where_sql：`/`event_type：`、`normalize_sql：`/`aggregate_sql：`/`keep_sql：` 的机器行值——形态合法但取值被改写的漂移（如 `where_sql` 改筛 `pending`、`keep_sql` 改 `repo_id = repo_id`）一律非零退出；本生成器经 ③ 的守卫调用继承全部冻结核验）。**R64 增一条：**⑦ repo 归属谓词（`keep_sql`，单点事实源）一致应用于全部五条查询——谓词通过行总数探测、全量违规聚合、实例化事件数、映射成功输入行数与 keyset 游标页的 WHERE 均含 `AND (<keep_sql>)`（异 repo 行不进入分母、窗口与违规计数，也不能占满窗口排挤本 repo 行；资格与保留同一谓词，消除 R64 反例：30 本 repo 合规行 + 10 异 repo 行覆盖率被稀释为 75%、10,000 异 repo 行排挤使窗口成功为 0），投影列 = 该来源全部字典字段（游标页首列为 `rowid`——仅分页游标元数据，用于推进游标与递增断言，不进入事件编码与产物（R65-P2-1 澄清）；其余投影仅为原值字节长 `length(CAST(<f> AS BLOB))`，不计算归一化式、不读原值）。**R66 增两条：**⑧ 机器行取值只认顶格书写或顶格以 `- ` / `* ` 列表标记起首的行（`^(- |\* )?`；四空格及以上缩进属缩进代码块，其中的示例行不参与取值与计数，R66-P1-2，与 A.6/A.7 同口径）。⑨ 第 3/4 条为聚合统计查询——返回标量计数、不向客户端物化行；窗口内（≤ `collect.max_rows`）逐行归一/编码在 SQLite 内执行且受 ADR-DC-02 第 4 条总解码预算硬约束，属该 ADR 第 1 条登记的有界例外（R66-P1-3）；生产增量分页形态由第 5 条 keyset 游标页（每页 ≤ 1,000 行）演示，测量与生产在窗口边界与游标口径上一致。同一输入逐字节同输出（重生成可比对的确定性）。

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
src="${1:?usage: a13_measure_gen.sh <source name> [file]}"
f="${2:-docs/development/plan/issues/468.md}"
[ -f "$f" ] || { echo "FAIL: plan file missing"; exit 2; }
bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a5_dictionary_guard.sh" "$f" >&2 || { echo "FAIL: dictionary drift or invalid (A.5 re-check before generation)" >&2; exit 1; }
bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a6_predicates_guard.sh" "$src" "$f" >&2 || { echo "FAIL: predicates drift or invalid for $src (A.6 re-check before generation)" >&2; exit 1; }
bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a7_rules_guard.sh" "$f" >&2 || { echo "FAIL: rules drift or invalid (A.7 re-check before generation)" >&2; exit 1; }
fv=$(bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a12_fence_view.sh" "$f")
sec=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^## 契约草案$/{ in_s=1; next } /^## / && in_s { exit } in_s')
pred=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$sec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^### 行资格谓词$/{f=1;next} f && /^### / && $0!="### agent_session" && $0!="### operation v2"{exit} f')
block=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$pred" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk -v h="### ${src}" '$0 == h { f=1; next } /^### / && f { exit } f')
[ -n "$block" ] || { echo "FAIL: predicate block missing for $src"; exit 1; }
tbl=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^(- |\* )?table：[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]/{ sub(/^(- |\* )?table：[[:space:]]*/, ""); print; exit }')
[ -n "$tbl" ] || { echo "FAIL: table： line missing for $src"; exit 1; }
case "$tbl" in *[!A-Za-z0-9_]*) echo "FAIL: table not a bare identifier: $tbl"; exit 1 ;; esac
case "$src" in
  agent_session) [ "$tbl" = "agent_session" ] || { echo "FAIL: table must be agent_session for $src: $tbl"; exit 1; } ;;
  'operation v2') [ "$tbl" = "operation" ] || { echo "FAIL: table must be operation for $src: $tbl"; exit 1; } ;;
esac
wh=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^(- |\* )?where_sql：[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]/{ sub(/^(- |\* )?where_sql：[[:space:]]*/, ""); print; exit }')
[ -n "$wh" ] || { echo "FAIL: where_sql： line missing for $src"; exit 1; }
et=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$block" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^(- |\* )?event_type：[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]/{ sub(/^(- |\* )?event_type：[[:space:]]*/, ""); print; exit }')
[ -n "$et" ] || { echo "FAIL: event_type： line missing for $src"; exit 1; }
case "$et" in *[!A-Za-z0-9_]*) echo "FAIL: event_type not a bare identifier: $et"; exit 1 ;; esac
ev="'$et'"
case "$src" in
  agent_session) col='agent_session / agent_checkpoint' ;;
  'operation v2') col='operation v2' ;;
  *) echo "FAIL: unknown source $src"; exit 2 ;;
esac
rules=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$sec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^### 精炼规则$/{f=1;next} /^### /&&f{exit} f')
mx=$(printf '%s\n' "$rules" | sed -nE 's/^collect\.max_rows = 10000$/10000/p')
[ "$mx" = "10000" ] || { echo "FAIL: collect.max_rows line missing or not 10000"; exit 1; }
nz=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^(- |\* )?normalize_sql：[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]/{ sub(/^(- |\* )?normalize_sql：[[:space:]]*/, ""); print; exit }')
[ -n "$nz" ] || { echo "FAIL: normalize_sql： line missing"; exit 1; }
ag=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^(- |\* )?aggregate_sql：[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]/{ sub(/^(- |\* )?aggregate_sql：[[:space:]]*/, ""); print; exit }')
[ -n "$ag" ] || { echo "FAIL: aggregate_sql： line missing"; exit 1; }
dz=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^(- |\* )?denoise_sql：[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]/{ sub(/^(- |\* )?denoise_sql：[[:space:]]*/, ""); print; exit }')
[ "$dz" = "DISTINCT" ] || { echo "FAIL: denoise_sql must be exactly DISTINCT (v1 fixed semantics)"; exit 1; }
kp=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$rules" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^(- |\* )?keep_sql：[[:space:]]*[^[:space:]]/{ sub(/^(- |\* )?keep_sql：[[:space:]]*/, ""); print; exit }')
[ -n "$kp" ] || { echo "FAIL: keep_sql： line missing"; exit 1; }
dict=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$sec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^### 字段字典$/{f=1;next} /^### /&&f{exit} f')
rows=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$dict" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '$0=="| 字段名 | 类型 | 必填性 | 来源事实面 | 来源锚点 | 最大字节 |"{f=1;next} f && /^\|/{print} f && !/^\|/{exit}')
[ -n "$rows" ] || { echo "FAIL: dictionary table missing"; exit 1; }
kpref=
while IFS= read -r r; do
  if [ -z "$r" ]; then continue; fi
  fn=$(printf '%s' "$r" | cut -d'|' -f2 | sed 's/^ *//;s/ *$//')
  if { trap '' PIPE; printf '%s' "$kp" 2>/dev/null || true; } | sed "s/'[^']*'//g" | awk -v f="$fn" '{ n=split($0, tok, /[^A-Za-z0-9_]+/); for (i=1; i<=n; i++) if (tok[i]==f) found=1 } END{ exit !found }'; then kpref=1; fi
done <<< "$rows"
[ -n "$kpref" ] || { echo "FAIL: keep_sql references no dictionary field: $kp"; exit 1; }
for pair in "keep:$kp" "normalize:$nz" "aggregate:$ag"; do
  frag=${pair#*:}
  if [ "${pair%%:*}" = keep ]; then allow_v=0; else allow_v=1; fi
  for tok in $(printf '%s' "$frag" | sed "s/'[^']*'//g" | tr -s ' ' | sed 's/(SELECT value FROM config_kv WHERE key = ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1)//g' | tr -c 'A-Za-z0-9_' ' '); do
    case "$tok" in
      AND|OR|NOT|NULL|IS|IN|LIKE|GLOB|BETWEEN|CASE|WHEN|THEN|ELSE|END|CAST|AS|TEXT|BLOB|INTEGER|REAL|length|ifnull|coalesce|trim|lower|upper|abs|DISTINCT|TRUE|FALSE|true|false) continue ;;
      and|or|not|null|is|in|like|glob|between|case|when|then|else|end|cast|as|text|blob|integer|real) continue ;;
      _v) [ "$allow_v" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: keep_sql must reference source columns, not _v: $frag"; exit 1; }; continue ;;
    esac
    ok=0
    while IFS= read -r r; do
      if [ -z "$r" ]; then continue; fi
      fn=$(printf '%s' "$r" | cut -d'|' -f2 | sed 's/^ *//;s/ *$//')
      if [ "$tok" = "$fn" ]; then ok=1; fi
    done <<< "$rows"
    [ "$ok" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: fragment references non-dictionary identifier $tok: $frag"; exit 1; }
  done
done
vio=
proj=
lproj=   # R68-P1-3：字段循环前显式初始化，避免环境污染变量（如 `lproj=working_dir` 把原生路径列混入游标页投影）使页码查询带上非契约列
while IFS= read -r r; do
  if [ -z "$r" ]; then continue; fi
  n=$(printf '%s' "$r" | cut -d'|' -f2 | sed 's/^ *//;s/ *$//')
  ty=$(printf '%s' "$r" | cut -d'|' -f3 | sed 's/^ *//;s/ *$//')
  rq=$(printf '%s' "$r" | cut -d'|' -f4 | sed 's/^ *//;s/ *$//')
  sc=$(printf '%s' "$r" | cut -d'|' -f5 | sed 's/^ *//;s/ *$//')
  cap=$(printf '%s' "$r" | cut -d'|' -f7 | sed 's/^ *//;s/ *$//')
  if [ "$sc" != "$col" ]; then continue; fi
  nzf=$(printf '%s' "$nz" | sed "s/_v/$n/g")
  # R67-P1-1：归一化值判定用 gated 形式——原值超字节上限（应为违规）时归一化式不被计算/物化，用 CASE 短路由经原值判长；否则 `OR` 仍会物化超限 trim(_v)，破坏 ADR-DC-02 第 4 条总解码预算
  nzv="CASE WHEN length(CAST(($n) AS BLOB)) > $cap THEN NULL ELSE ($nzf) END"
  proj="${proj:+$proj, }$n"
  core="(($nzv) IS NOT NULL AND length(CAST(($nzv) AS BLOB)) > $cap)"
  case "$ty" in
    计数) core="$core OR (($nzv) IS NOT NULL AND (length(CAST(($nzv) AS BLOB)) = 0 OR CAST(($nzv) AS TEXT) GLOB '*[^0-9]*' OR instr(CAST(($nzv) AS TEXT), char(0)) > 0))" ;;  # R76-P2-3：计数 TEXT 可含内嵌 NUL（`1\0x` 经 GLOB 仍过），`instr(...,char(0))` 拒之
    布尔) core="$core OR (($nzv) IS NOT NULL AND CAST(($nzv) AS TEXT) NOT IN ('0','1'))" ;;
    时间戳) core="$core OR (($nzv) IS NOT NULL AND (length(CAST(($nzv) AS TEXT)) = 0 OR CAST(($nzv) AS TEXT) GLOB '*[^0-9]*' OR instr(CAST(($nzv) AS TEXT), char(0)) > 0 OR length(CAST(($nzv) AS TEXT)) > 15))" ;;
    id|digest)
      # R77-P1-1 移入 case（conds/vio 在其后构建）：空/全白归一化只、authority 内 @ 超 7 计违规
      core="$core OR (($nzv) IS NOT NULL AND length(CAST(($nzv) AS TEXT)) = 0)"
      authx="substr(($nzv), instr(($nzv),'://')+3)"
      rd="min(CASE WHEN instr($authx,'/')>0 THEN instr($authx,'/') ELSE 999999 END, CASE WHEN instr($authx,'?')>0 THEN instr($authx,'?') ELSE 999999 END, CASE WHEN instr($authx,'#')>0 THEN instr($authx,'#') ELSE 999999 END)"
      authb="substr($authx,1,$rd-1)"
      nx="length($authb) - length(replace($authb,'@',''))"
      core="$core OR (($nzv) IS NOT NULL AND instr(($nzv),'://')>0 AND ($nx) > 7)"
      ;;  # R73-P1-2 补内嵌 NUL 拒绝：`123\0secret` 在 dig 检查下仍过，`instr(...,char(0))` 拒之  # R71-P1-5/R72-P1-4：时间戳须为非负十进数字符串（≤15 位，毫秒范围）；`bad`/`-1` 不计为合法
  esac
  if [ "$ty" = "枚举" ]; then
    dom=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$sec" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^### 枚举值域$/{f=1;next} /^### /&&f{exit} f')
    stats=$(printf '%s\n' "$dom" | awk -F'|' -v f="$n" 'NF { nm=$2; gsub(/^ +| +$/,"",nm); if (nm==f) { v=$3; gsub(/^ +| +$/,"",v); tot++; if (v != "") ne++ } } END { print tot+0, ne+0 }')
    [ "$stats" = "1 1" ] || { echo "FAIL: enum field $n must have exactly one 枚举值域 row with a non-empty value (rows/non-empty = $stats; missing/duplicate/empty domain row would silently drop the NOT IN check)"; exit 1; }
    vals=$(printf '%s\n' "$dom" | awk -F'|' -v f="$n" 'NF { nm=$2; gsub(/^ +| +$/,"",nm); if (nm==f) { v=$3; gsub(/^ +| +$/,"",v); print v } }')
    q=$(printf '%s' "$vals" | sed "s/'/''/g" | sed "s|/|','|g")
    core="$core OR (($nzv) IS NOT NULL AND ($nzv) NOT IN ('$q'))"
  fi
  if [ "$rq" = "必填" ]; then
    conds="(($n) IS NULL OR length(CAST(($n) AS BLOB)) > $cap OR ($nzv) IS NULL OR $core)"
  else
    conds="(($n) IS NOT NULL AND (length(CAST(($n) AS BLOB)) > $cap OR $core))"
  fi
  vio="${vio:+$vio OR }$conds"
  lproj="${lproj:+$lproj, }length(CAST(($n) AS BLOB))"
  # R70-P1-9：对 id/digest 类型字段，事件编码前经 redact_url_credentials 口径剥离 URL 用户信息（`scheme://user:pass@host` -> `scheme://host`），防止凭据进入事件文本
  if [ "$ty" = "时间戳" ]; then
    rv="(CASE WHEN CAST(($nzv) AS INTEGER) < 100000000000 THEN CAST(($nzv) AS INTEGER)*1000 ELSE ($nzv) END)"  # R71-P1-5：时间戳统一编码为毫秒（秒值 <1e11 则 ×1000；毫秒原样），并受上一条非负整数值域校验
  elif [ "$ty" = "id" ] || [ "$ty" = "digest" ]; then
    # R76-P1-1/P1-5：id/digest 为不透明非空标识符——归一化后为空（含全空白 `op_id`）计违规；authority 内 @ 超过 7 个（超出脱敏能力）计违规（fail-closed）；计数须限定在 authority 内（`://` 之后、首个 `/ ? #` 之前）
    core="$core OR (($nzv) IS NOT NULL AND length(CAST(($nzv) AS TEXT)) = 0)"
    authx="substr(($nzv), instr(($nzv),'://')+3)"
    rd="min(CASE WHEN instr($authx,'/')>0 THEN instr($authx,'/') ELSE 999999 END, CASE WHEN instr($authx,'?')>0 THEN instr($authx,'?') ELSE 999999 END, CASE WHEN instr($authx,'#')>0 THEN instr($authx,'#') ELSE 999999 END)"
    authb="substr($authx,1,$rd-1)"
    nx="length($authb) - length(replace($authb,'@',''))"
    core="$core OR (($nzv) IS NOT NULL AND instr(($nzv),'://')>0 AND ($nx) > 7)"
    rv="CASE WHEN instr(($nzv),'://')>0 AND instr(substr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),1,min(CASE WHEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'/')>0 THEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'/') ELSE 999999 END, CASE WHEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'?')>0 THEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'?') ELSE 999999 END, CASE WHEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'#')>0 THEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'#') ELSE 999999 END)-1),'@')>0 THEN substr(($nzv),1,instr(($nzv),'://')+2) || substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3+COALESCE(NULLIF(instr(substr(substr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),1,min(CASE WHEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'/')>0 THEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'/') ELSE 999999 END, CASE WHEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'?')>0 THEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'?') ELSE 999999 END, CASE WHEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'#')>0 THEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'#') ELSE 999999 END)-1),COALESCE(NULLIF(instr(substr(substr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),1,min(CASE WHEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'/')>0 THEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'/') ELSE 999999 END, CASE WHEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'?')>0 THEN instr(substr(($nzv),instr(($nzv),'://')+3),'?') [ -n "$proj" ] || { echo "FAIL: no fields for $src in dictionary"; exit 1; }
printf 'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM %s WHERE (%s) AND (%s)\n' "$tbl" "$wh" "$kp"
printf 'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM %s WHERE (%s) AND (%s) AND (%s)\n' "$tbl" "$wh" "$kp" "$vio"
aggkey=$(printf '%s' "$ag" | sed 's/_v/_ev/g')
printf 'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (SELECT DISTINCT (%s) FROM (SELECT DISTINCT %s AS _ev FROM %s WHERE (%s) AND (%s) AND rowid IN (SELECT rowid FROM %s WHERE (%s) AND (%s) ORDER BY rowid LIMIT %s) AND NOT (%s)))\n' "$aggkey" "$ev" "$tbl" "$wh" "$kp" "$tbl" "$wh" "$kp" "$mx" "$vio"
printf 'SELECT COUNT(*) FROM %s WHERE (%s) AND (%s) AND rowid IN (SELECT rowid FROM %s WHERE (%s) AND (%s) ORDER BY rowid LIMIT %s) AND NOT (%s)\n' "$tbl" "$wh" "$kp" "$tbl" "$wh" "$kp" "$mx" "$vio"
printf 'SELECT rowid, %s FROM %s WHERE (%s) AND (%s) AND rowid > __CURSOR__ ORDER BY rowid LIMIT __PAGELIMIT__\n' "$lproj" "$tbl" "$wh" "$kp"
```

### A.14 `a14_measure_run.sh`（测量运行器；非门禁，DC-05/DC-10 测量序列消费）

参数：`$1` = 来源名；`$2` = 快照副本路径；可选 `$3` = 目标文件。行为：**先以 A.4（契约骨架）+ A.5（字典/枚举值域/预算/锚点排他）+ A.6（两来源谓词）+ A.7（精炼规则）重验「契约草案」节——前置卡之后的任一契约漂移（含 A.5 不覆盖的 `where_sql` / 规则机器行漂移，如被注入 `random()`）即 fail-closed，不得在漂移契约上产出虚假计数（R61 起 A.5、R62 扩为全组；全部守卫诊断重定向 stderr，stdout 恰为八条证据行）**；再经 A.13 重生成该来源的 SQL（确定性），经 stdin 以 `printf '%s' "$sql" | sqlite3 -readonly` 逐条执行（R74-P2-1：大 SQL 避免 argv 上限、不经 shell 再解析）；`collect.max_rows` 只接受整行恰为 `collect.max_rows = 10000`（与 A.5 预算、A.8 截断判定同一事实源）。输出证据行八条（固定顺序）：`成功行数：<N>`（映射成功输入行数 = A.13 第 4 条查询：窗口内、契约合规、`keep_sql` 保留谓词过滤后的输入行数，未去重）、`分母：本仓库真实 <N> 行`（窗口后）、`覆盖率：<0..100>%` 或零分母 `覆盖率：N/A（0/0）`、`契约违规：<N>`（全量聚合）、`实例化事件数：<N>`（A.13 第 3 条查询：归一/聚合真实执行 + DISTINCT 去噪后的去重聚合事件数，≤ 成功行数）、`window_truncated=true|false`（总数 > 上限即截断）、`实际总数：<N>`、`样本充足` / `真实样本不足 → DC-07 须判 INCONCLUSIVE`（分母 <30 判定）。分页实例化核验：执行第 5 条游标页 SQL 的有界循环（首页以 `1=1` 替代游标条件以覆盖负 rowid；之后游标严格递增；每页上限 min(1000, 分母−已计)；累计页行数恰等于窗口分母；超 11 页即越界拒绝）。DC-05/DC-10 的测量序列在同一副本上双跑本运行器并断言两次输出逐行一致，一致后逐字登记入证据块数值行。

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
src="${1:?usage: a14_measure_run.sh <source name> <snapshot.db> [file]}"
snap="${2:?usage: a14_measure_run.sh <source name> <snapshot.db> [file]}"
f="${3:-docs/development/plan/issues/468.md}"
[ -f "$f" ] || { echo "FAIL: plan file missing"; exit 2; }
[ -f "$snap" ] || { echo "FAIL: snapshot missing: $snap"; exit 2; }
bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a4_contract_skeleton_guard.sh" "$f" >&2 || { echo "FAIL: contract skeleton drift (A.4 re-check before measurement)" >&2; exit 1; }
bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a5_dictionary_guard.sh" "$f" >&2 || { echo "FAIL: dictionary drift (A.5 re-check before measurement)" >&2; exit 1; }
bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a6_predicates_guard.sh" agent_session "$f" >&2 || { echo "FAIL: predicates drift (A.6 agent_session re-check before measurement)" >&2; exit 1; }
bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a6_predicates_guard.sh" 'operation v2' "$f" >&2 || { echo "FAIL: predicates drift (A.6 operation v2 re-check before measurement)" >&2; exit 1; }
bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a7_rules_guard.sh" "$f" >&2 || { echo "FAIL: rules drift (A.7 re-check before measurement)" >&2; exit 1; }
gen=$(bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a13_measure_gen.sh" "$src" "$f")
total_sql=$(printf '%s\n' "$gen" | sed -n '1p')
viof_sql=$(printf '%s\n' "$gen" | sed -n '2p')
inst_sql=$(printf '%s\n' "$gen" | sed -n '3p')
map_sql=$(printf '%s\n' "$gen" | sed -n '4p')
page_sql=$(printf '%s\n' "$gen" | sed -n '5p')
[ -n "$total_sql" ] && [ -n "$viof_sql" ] && [ -n "$inst_sql" ] && [ -n "$map_sql" ] && [ -n "$page_sql" ] || { echo "FAIL: generator output incomplete"; exit 1; }
sq() { printf '%s' "$1" | sqlite3 -batch -noheader -readonly "$snap"; }  # R72-P1-3：大 SQL 经 stdin 传入，避免 argv 上限
intcheck() { case "$1" in ''|*[!0-9]*) echo "FAIL: expected a non-negative integer but got: '$1'"; exit 1 ;; esac; }
# R73-P1-3：测量前核验 repo 身份存在（`config_kv` 的 `libra.repoid` 缺失/为空 → 计数全 0 会伪装成样本不足；运行时解析器 `workspace.rs:549` 亦 fail-closed）
# R73-P1-3/R74-P1-2：先核验 repo 身份存在且非空、无填充/空白（**在 SQL 侧校验**——shell `$()` 会剥掉存储的尾部换行，`repo\n` 在 shell 变量里被当作合法；SQL `value=trim(value, char(32)||char(9)||char(10)||char(13))` 会拒绝含尾随换行的值，等价于运行时解析器 workspace.rs:569 的拒绝）
idvalid=$(printf '%s' "SELECT CASE WHEN value IS NOT NULL AND length(trim(value, char(32)||char(9)||char(10)||char(11)||char(12)||char(13)||char(133)||char(160)||char(5760)||char(8192)||char(8193)||char(8194)||char(8195)||char(8196)||char(8197)||char(8198)||char(8199)||char(8200)||char(8201)||char(8202)||char(8232)||char(8233)||char(8239)||char(8287)||char(12288))) > 0 AND value = trim(value, char(32)||char(9)||char(10)||char(11)||char(12)||char(13)||char(133)||char(160)||char(5760)||char(8192)||char(8193)||char(8194)||char(8195)||char(8196)||char(8197)||char(8198)||char(8199)||char(8200)||char(8201)||char(8202)||char(8232)||char(8233)||char(8239)||char(8287)||char(12288)) THEN 1 ELSE 0 END FROM config_kv WHERE key='libra.repoid' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1" | sqlite3 -batch -noheader -readonly "$snap")  # R76-P1-4：把 NBSP(U+00A0) 及常见 Unicode 空白纳入 trim——运行时解析器 workspace.rs:562 拒绝此类填充
[ "$idvalid" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: libra.repoid identity missing/empty/padded/whitespace in snapshot (runtime resolver workspace.rs:569 亦 reject; R74-P1-2)"; exit 1; }
total=$(sq "$total_sql"); intcheck "$total"
viofull=$(sq "$viof_sql"); intcheck "$viofull"
fv=$(bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a12_fence_view.sh" "$f")
mx=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^## 契约草案$/{ in_s=1; next } /^## / && in_s { exit } in_s' | awk '/^### 精炼规则$/{f=1;next} /^### /&&f{exit} f' | sed -nE 's/^collect\.max_rows = 10000$/10000/p')
[ "$mx" = "10000" ] || { echo "FAIL: collect.max_rows line missing or not 10000"; exit 1; }
if [ "$total" -gt "$mx" ]; then flag=true; den=$mx; else flag=false; den=$total; fi
nev=$(sq "$inst_sql"); intcheck "$nev"
ok=$(sq "$map_sql"); intcheck "$ok"
cur=0; acc=0; pages=0
while [ "$acc" -lt "$den" ]; do
  lim=$((den - acc)); if [ "$lim" -gt 1000 ]; then lim=1000; fi
  if [ "$pages" -eq 0 ]; then
    q=$(printf '%s' "$page_sql" | sed 's/AND rowid > __CURSOR__/AND 1=1/' | sed "s/__PAGELIMIT__/$lim/")
  else
    q=$(printf '%s' "$page_sql" | sed "s/__CURSOR__/$cur/" | sed "s/__PAGELIMIT__/$lim/")
  fi
  out=$(sq "$q")
  cnt=$(printf '%s\n' "$out" | sed '/^$/d' | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
  intcheck "$cnt"
  [ "$cnt" -le "$lim" ] || { echo "FAIL: page row count $cnt > page limit $lim"; exit 1; }
  if [ "$cnt" -eq 0 ]; then echo "FAIL: window short: paged $acc < denominator $den"; exit 1; fi
  last=$(printf '%s\n' "$out" | tail -1 | cut -d'|' -f1)
  { trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$last" 2>/dev/null || true; } | grep -qE '^-?[0-9]+$' || { echo "FAIL: page cursor not an integer: $last"; exit 1; }
  if [ "$pages" -gt 0 ] && [ "$last" -le "$cur" ]; then echo "FAIL: cursor not increasing: $last <= $cur"; exit 1; fi
  acc=$((acc + cnt)); pages=$((pages + 1)); cur=$last
  if [ "$pages" -gt 11 ]; then echo "FAIL: page loop unbounded (> 11 pages over 10000-row cap)"; exit 1; fi
done
[ "$acc" -eq "$den" ] || { echo "FAIL: paged rows $acc != denominator $den (R67-P1-8: loop must end exactly at the window denominator)"; exit 1; }
if [ "$den" -gt 0 ]; then
  cov=$(( (100 * ok + den / 2) / den ))
  covline="覆盖率：${cov}%"
else
  ok=0
  covline='覆盖率：N/A（0/0）'
fi
if [ "$den" -lt 30 ]; then mark='真实样本不足 → DC-07 须判 INCONCLUSIVE'; else mark='样本充足'; fi
printf '成功行数：%s\n分母：本仓库真实 %s 行\n%s\n契约违规：%s\n实例化事件数：%s\nwindow_truncated=%s\n实际总数：%s\n%s\n' "$ok" "$den" "$covline" "$viofull" "$nev" "$flag" "$total" "$mark"
```

### A.15 `a15_baseline_guard.sh`（M0 基线守卫；全局强制门，不计入任务卡特有 AC/VER）

可选参数 `$1` = 目标文件（默认 `docs/development/plan/issues/468.md`）。用途（R64 增；R65/R66/R67 修订）：冻结比对（A.5/A.6/A.7 的常量与契约正文同在可修改的本文件中）存在「同提交改写契约正文与守卫常量」的假绿通道——本守卫把「附录 A」（全部门禁脚本与冻结常量）锚定到 **M0 基线提交**（本计划获批修订的不可变基线，DC-01 开工时以 `libra rev-parse HEAD` 填入「执行检查必备需求（强制）」节的「M0 基线提交」行，并以 DC-01 的 A.2 父基线断言与 M0==base 机械互核绑定）：从本文件读出 M0 SHA（形态恰一个、40 位小写十六进制，缺失/重复/非法即 fail-closed——pre-M0 状态下本门对卡片 fail-closed），断言其为 `HEAD` 祖先（`libra merge-base --is-ancestor`），取该提交中本文件 blob，**以保留 EOF 原始字节的流式方式逐字节比较**两侧「附录 A」节（`## 附录 A：门禁脚本` 起至文件尾）——blob 经 `mktemp` 落盘后，两侧各自以 `tail -n +<标题行号>` 流式输入 `cmp`（R66-P1-1：`tail` 保留文件尾部原始字节；此前 `awk '{s=1} s'` 提取会给末行补换行，仅差尾部换行有无的两侧会被误判一致）——不经命令替换（命令替换会剥掉尾部全部换行再只补一个，尾部多换行的同文两侧会被误判漂移，R65-P1-13 的确定性假红）。**固定副本执行（R64-P1-2 引入；R69 校正引用）：** 本守卫即附录校验器，不得取自被校验的同一可编辑面（同次改动把脚本改成 `exit 0` 即形成确定性假绿）——「执行检查必备需求（强制）」节的门命令从 **M0 基线提交 blob** 提取本脚本与 A.12 到独立 pin 目录执行（基线 blob 自 M0 起不可变；当前工作树对本脚本或 A.12 的任何改写——含中性化——都被基线副本的漂移比对捕获）；校验器自身的轮换信任锚即 M0 轮换协议（新基线 blob 的校验器版本已经该修订的复审 PASS）。**轮换链反向走查（R65 增；R66/R67 加固）：** 守卫自当前 M0 逐环反向核验——读取每环提交 blob，**只在其 `## 执行检查必备需求（强制）` 节内（标题精确匹配并在当前文件与每环 blob 各断言唯一——R67 附带（与 R67-P0-1 同批）：前缀匹配会把 `## 执行检查必备需求示例` 类仿冒节的伪 M0 行选入）经围栏感知视图解析恰一行的「M0 基线提交」行**（R66-P0-1：节内唯一解析使节外伪行不可见）：该行或**逐字等于冻结的完整占位行**（本脚本内 `placeholder` 常量，与「执行检查必备需求（强制）」节的 pre-M0 行逐字一致——R67-P0-2：此前仅子串匹配 `（待 DC-01 开工填入`，`- **M0 基线提交：** nonsense（待 DC-01 开工填入` 即冒充链终止；链尾对原始批准基线的绑定由 DC-01 的 M0==base 机械互核与逐环连续性共同提供）则走查终止、链合法；否则该值必须是合法的 40 位小写十六进制、是当前环的真祖先（禁止自环），且本文件「修订历史」必须存在**恰一条**含完整绑定形态 `M0 轮换：<上一环 SHA> -> <当前环 SHA>（R<n> PASS）` 的表格行（SHA 对与轮次须在同一行内绑定，拼接/重复行拒绝——R67 附带：此前先 `grep -F` 找 SHA 对、再贪婪 `sed` 从整行另一处提轮次，可错绑 PASS 轮次），并且「Codex review log」存在 `R<n>` 的 `PASS` 行——**无轮换记录的指针改写一律 fail-closed**（静默改指另一个附录相同的祖先提交，如 DC-01 自己的提交，会被此走查拒绝：其 blob 的节内 M0 行不是占位行且无对应轮换记录；R65-P1-4）。**评审证据须为已提交且不可重放的记录（R66-P0-2；R67 附带加固）：** 被引用的 `R<n> PASS` 行必须存在于**轮换目标提交自身的 blob** 的「Codex review log」节内（附录变更提交在形成时——轮换协议第 ② 步——已携带该轮复审 PASS 记录；只在当前可编辑文件里补写轮换行与虚构 PASS 行无法通过），且**不得存在于轮换目标提交 `cur` 的直接父提交 `cur~1` 的 blob review log 节内**（R70-P1-2：`prev` 与 `cur` 间可能存在中间提交，旧 PASS 行可在中间提交被加入、被后续轮换继承重放；绑定到 `cur~1` 才确认 PASS 恰好新增于 `cur`；`cur` 无父（根提交）则继承不可能、跳过）。自上一基线继承的旧 PASS 行不构成新证据，每次轮换要求目标侧历史中新提交的、在 `cur~1` blob 中计数为零的 PASS 记录（继承重放拒绝）。走查上限 8 环（超出即拒绝）。**M0 基线轮换（R65-P1-5，协议预登记于「执行检查必备需求（强制）」节）：** 附录 A 确需变更时，先修订本计划并取得 Codex 复审 `PASS`（记录于 review log），附录变更提交保持 M0 行不变且必须已包含该 PASS 记录（其提交前本门预期以「appendix drifted」分支失败——该失败即守卫被正确触发的证据，任何其它分支失败即停止），紧随的轮换提交为纯记账提交（只改 M0 行指向附录变更提交、登记 `M0 轮换` 修订行、同步 `plan-status.md`），轮换提交的本门通过后才可推送，两笔提交在同一推送窗口连续推送。

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
f="${1:-docs/development/plan/issues/468.md}"
[ -f "$f" ] || { echo "FAIL: plan file missing"; exit 2; }
fv=$(bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a12_fence_view.sh" "$f")
[ "$(printf '%s\n' "$fv" | grep -cFx '## 执行检查必备需求（强制）')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: 执行检查必备需求（强制） heading not exactly once (exact-match; prefix lookalikes such as '## 执行检查必备需求示例' do not count)"; exit 1; }
esec=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '$0=="## 执行检查必备需求（强制）"{s=1;next} /^## /&&s{exit} s')
[ -n "$esec" ] || { echo "FAIL: 执行检查必备需求（强制） section missing"; exit 1; }
[ "$(printf '%s\n' "$esec" | grep -cE '^- \*\*M0 基线提交：\*\*' || true)" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: M0 基线提交 prefix not exactly once in 执行检查必备需求（强制） section"; exit 1; }
line=$(printf '%s\n' "$esec" | grep -E '^- \*\*M0 基线提交：\*\*')
m0=${line#'- **M0 基线提交：** `'}
case "$line" in
  '- **M0 基线提交：** `'*'`') : ;;
  *) echo "FAIL: M0 line malformed (must be exactly - **M0 基线提交：** \`<40-hex>\` with no trailing content): $line"; exit 1 ;;
esac
m0=${m0%'`'}
[ "${#m0}" = "40" ] || { echo "FAIL: M0 SHA not 40-hex (DC-01 fills it at card start; pre-M0 state is fail-closed)"; exit 1; }
case "$m0" in *[!0-9a-f]*) echo "FAIL: M0 SHA not lowercase hex: $m0"; exit 1 ;; esac
libra merge-base --is-ancestor "$m0" HEAD || { echo "FAIL: M0 SHA $m0 is not an ancestor of HEAD"; exit 1; }
tmpb=$(mktemp)
trap 'rm -f "$tmpb"' EXIT
tmpb2=$(mktemp)
trap 'rm -f "$tmpb" "$tmpb2"' EXIT
libra cat-file -p "${m0}:docs/development/plan/issues/468.md" > "$tmpb" || { echo "FAIL: cannot read plan file blob at M0 SHA $m0"; exit 1; }
[ "$(grep -cFx '## 附录 A：门禁脚本' "$tmpb" || true)" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: appendix A heading missing or not unique in M0 blob"; exit 1; }
[ "$(grep -cFx '## 附录 A：门禁脚本' "$f" || true)" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: appendix A heading missing or not unique in current file"; exit 1; }
bn=$(grep -n -Fx '## 附录 A：门禁脚本' "$tmpb" | cut -d: -f1)
cn=$(grep -n -Fx '## 附录 A：门禁脚本' "$f" | cut -d: -f1)
cmp -s <(tail -n +"$bn" "$tmpb") <(tail -n +"$cn" "$f") || { echo "FAIL: appendix A (guard scripts and frozen constants) drifted from M0 baseline — guard/contract changes require plan revision and re-review first"; exit 1; }
rsec=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^### 修订历史$/{s=1;next} /^## /&&s{exit} /^### /&&s{exit} s')
lsec=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$fv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^## Codex review log$/{s=1;next} /^## /&&s{exit} s')
placeholder='- **M0 基线提交：** `（待 DC-01 开工填入：以 libra rev-parse HEAD 固定 M0 批准链末端提交——即 DC-01 的父提交——并同批写入本卡 Verification 记录）`'
# R70-P1-6：把 M0 指针绑定到「最新记录的轮换目标」——若 修订历史 存在 `M0 轮换：<prev> -> <target>`，则当前 m0 必须等于最新 target；否则回退到旧基线会让链尾占位通过、漏掉后续轮换
# R71-P1-1/P1-11：轮换记录须为「修订历史」中的规范表行，且形成线性链（每条记录的 target == 下一条的 source），并把 M0 绑定到链的**末端** target；无轮换记录（首个 post-M0 卡）则 latestrot 为空、跳过目标校验
rots=$(printf '%s\n' "$rsec" | grep -E '^\|.*M0 轮换：[0-9a-f]{40} -> [0-9a-f]{40}（R[0-9]+ PASS）' || echo '')  # R72-P1-1：无轮换记录时 set -e 不退出（rots 为空即跳过轮换校验）
if [ -n "$rots" ]; then
  prev_target=""; last_target=""
  while IFS= read -r line; do
    src=$(printf '%s' "$line" | sed -nE 's/.*M0 轮换：([0-9a-f]{40}) -> [0-9a-f]{40}.*/\1/p')
    tgt=$(printf '%s' "$line" | sed -nE 's/.*M0 轮换：[0-9a-f]{40} -> ([0-9a-f]{40}).*/\1/p')
    [ -n "$src" ] && [ -n "$tgt" ] || { echo "FAIL: malformed M0 rotation table row: $line"; exit 1; }
    if [ -n "$prev_target" ]; then
      [ "$src" = "$prev_target" ] || { echo "FAIL: M0 rotation chain not linear ($src != $prev_target); R71-P1-11"; exit 1; }
    fi
    prev_target="$tgt"; last_target="$tgt"
  done <<< "$rots"
  [ "$m0" = "$last_target" ] || { echo "FAIL: M0 pointer $m0 != terminal rotation target $last_target (R70-P1-6/R71-P1-11：回退到旧基线可令链尾占位通过；须指向链末端)"; exit 1; }
fi
cur="$m0"
i=0
while :; do
  i=$((i+1))
  [ "$i" -le 8 ] || { echo "FAIL: M0 rotation chain too long (> 8 links)"; exit 1; }
  libra cat-file -p "${cur}:docs/development/plan/issues/468.md" > "$tmpb" || { echo "FAIL: cannot read plan file blob at chain SHA $cur"; exit 1; }
  bfv=$(bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a12_fence_view.sh" "$tmpb")
  [ "$(printf '%s\n' "$bfv" | grep -cFx '## 执行检查必备需求（强制）')" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: chain blob at $cur: 执行检查必备需求（强制） heading not exactly once (exact-match)"; exit 1; }
  besec=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$bfv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '$0=="## 执行检查必备需求（强制）"{s=1;next} /^## /&&s{exit} s')
  [ -n "$besec" ] || { echo "FAIL: chain blob at $cur lacks 执行检查必备需求（强制） section"; exit 1; }
  [ "$(printf '%s\n' "$besec" | grep -cE '^- \*\*M0 基线提交：\*\*' || true)" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: chain blob at $cur has M0 prefix not exactly once in its 执行检查必备需求（强制） section (out-of-section lookalikes are invisible to the chain walk)"; exit 1; }
  cl=$(printf '%s\n' "$besec" | grep -E '^- \*\*M0 基线提交：\*\*')
  if [ "$cl" = "$placeholder" ]; then
    echo "OK: appendix A identical to M0 baseline ($m0; rotation chain verified, $((i-1)) link(s) to placeholder era)"; exit 0
  fi
  case "$cl" in
    '- **M0 基线提交：** `'*'`') : ;;
    *) echo "FAIL: chain blob at $cur has malformed M0 line in its 执行检查必备需求（强制） section (must be the frozen placeholder line verbatim or exactly - **M0 基线提交：** \`<40-hex>\` with no trailing content)"; exit 1 ;;
  esac
  prev=${cl#'- **M0 基线提交：** `'}
  prev=${prev%'`'}
  [ "${#prev}" = "40" ] || { echo "FAIL: chain blob at $cur holds a non-40-hex M0 value"; exit 1; }
  case "$prev" in *[!0-9a-f]*) echo "FAIL: chain blob at $cur holds a non-lowercase-hex M0 value"; exit 1 ;; esac
  [ "$prev" != "$cur" ] || { echo "FAIL: M0 rotation chain self-loop at $cur"; exit 1; }
  libra merge-base --is-ancestor "$prev" "$cur" || { echo "FAIL: rotation source $prev is not an ancestor of rotation target $cur"; exit 1; }
  rotc=$(printf '%s\n' "$rsec" | grep -cE "^\| .*M0 轮换：${prev} -> ${cur}（R[0-9]+ PASS）" || true)
  [ "$rotc" = "1" ] || { echo "FAIL: rotation record ${prev} -> ${cur} not exactly once in full bound table-row form (SHA pair and round must bind within a single row; spliced or duplicated rows rejected)"; exit 1; }
  rnd=$(printf '%s\n' "$rsec" | sed -nE "s/^.*M0 轮换：${prev} -> ${cur}（(R[0-9]+) PASS）.*$/\1/p")
  [ -n "$rnd" ] || { echo "FAIL: rotation record ${prev} -> ${cur} lacks a (R<n> PASS) citation"; exit 1; }
  [ "$(printf '%s\n' "$lsec" | grep -cE "^\| ${rnd} \|[^|]*\| PASS \|" || true)" -ge 1 ] || { echo "FAIL: rotation record cites ${rnd} but no PASS row exists in Codex review log"; exit 1; }
  blsec=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$bfv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^## Codex review log$/{s=1;next} /^## /&&s{exit} s')
  [ -n "$blsec" ] || { echo "FAIL: chain blob at $cur lacks Codex review log section"; exit 1; }
  [ "$(printf '%s\n' "$blsec" | grep -cE "^\| ${rnd} \|[^|]*\| PASS \|" || true)" -ge 1 ] || { echo "FAIL: cited ${rnd} PASS row is not committed inside rotation-target blob $cur (review evidence must be an ancestor-committed record per rotation protocol step 2; rows written only into the current editable file do not count)"; exit 1; }
  # R69-P1-2：把评审证据绑定到「轮换目标提交（即附录变更提交）本身」——PASS 行须已提交于 cur 且不在 cur 的直接父提交 cur~1 中（而非仅不在旧基线 prev 中；prev 与 cur 之间可能存在中间提交，旧 PASS 可被继承重放）
  # R70-P1-7：`cur` 是有效的旋转目标提交，应必有父——`cur~1` 解析失败即 fail-closed（仅当 `cur` 确为根提交才跳过）；先以 `rev-list --parents -n 1` 判定。
  # R73-P2-4：轮换目标 `cur` 若为合并提交（多父），PASS 可能继承自任一父——逐个父检查，任一父含 PASS 即拒（根提交无父则跳过）
  parents=$(libra rev-list --parents -n 1 "$cur" | awk '{ for (i=2;i<=NF;i++) print $i }')
  if [ -z "$parents" ]; then
    echo "OK: ${rnd} PASS bound to rotation target ${cur} (root commit has no parent; inheritance impossible)"
  else
    inh=""
    for pp in $parents; do
      libra cat-file -p "${pp}:docs/development/plan/issues/468.md" > "$tmpb2" || { echo "FAIL: cannot read plan file blob at parent SHA $pp"; exit 1; }
      pfv=$(bash "$LIBRA_TEST_SCRATCH_DIR/issue468/a12_fence_view.sh" "$tmpb2")
      plsec=$({ trap '' PIPE; printf '%s\n' "$pfv" 2>/dev/null || true; } | awk '/^## Codex review log$/{s=1;next} /^## /&&s{exit} s')
      [ -n "$plsec" ] || { echo "FAIL: parent blob at $pp lacks Codex review log section"; exit 1; }
      n=$(printf '%s\n' "$plsec" | grep -cE "^\| ${rnd} \|[^|]*\| PASS \|" || true)
      [ "$n" = "0" ] || inh="$inh $pp"
    done
    [ -z "$inh" ] || { echo "FAIL: cited ${rnd} PASS row is inherited from parent(s)${inh} (must be newly added in rotation target ${cur}; R69-P1-2/R73-P2-4)"; exit 1; }
  fi
  cur="$prev"
done
```
